import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.Log.Basic
import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.ExpDeriv
import Mathlib.Tactic.NormNum
import Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith
import Mathlib.Tactic.FieldSimp
import Mathlib.Tactic.Ring
import Mathlib.Tactic.Convert

/- Chosen deterministic scenario: a uniformly mixed coffee in a constant 20 C
room. Time t is in minutes; k is an UNKNOWN effective loss rate in min^-1.
The exponential family is the modeling assumption, not measured evidence.
The factor 70 is the supplied initial 90 C minus the chosen ambient 20 C. -/
noncomputable def coffee (k t : ℝ) : ℝ := 20 + 70 * Real.exp (-k * t)

-- Every rate fits the supplied initial temperature; it contains no rate information.
lemma initial_temperature : ∀ k : ℝ, coffee k 0 = 90 := by
  intro k
  norm_num [coffee]

-- The chosen family satisfies Newton's cooling equation, with no extra premise.
lemma cooling_equation : ∀ k t : ℝ,
    HasDerivAt (coffee k) (-k * (coffee k t - 20)) t := by
  intro k t
  have h := (((hasDerivAt_id t).const_mul (-k)).exp.const_mul 70).const_add 20
  change HasDerivAt (coffee k)
    (70 * (Real.exp (-k * t) * (-k * 1))) t at h
  convert h using 1 <;> dsimp [coffee] <;> ring

lemma threshold_exponential :
    Real.exp (-Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ)) = (4 / 7 : ℝ) := by
  have h : Real.log (4 / 7 : ℝ) = -Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) := by
    rw [← Real.log_inv]
    norm_num
  rw [← h, Real.exp_log (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 4 / 7)]

-- Solving the observation equation: 60 C occurs at exactly log(7/4)/k minutes.
lemma threshold_time : ∀ k : ℝ, 0 < k → ∀ t : ℝ,
    (coffee k t = 60 ↔ t = Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / k) := by
  intro k hk t
  constructor
  · intro h
    have he : Real.exp (-k * t) = (4 / 7 : ℝ) := by
      dsimp [coffee] at h
      linarith
    have hx : -k * t = -Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) :=
      Real.exp_injective (he.trans threshold_exponential.symm)
    apply (eq_div_iff (ne_of_gt hk)).2
    nlinarith
  · intro h
    rw [h]
    have hx : -k * (Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / k) = -Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) := by
      field_simp
    unfold coffee
    rw [hx, threshold_exponential]
    norm_num

-- At or below the target iff the threshold time has passed: this is a first crossing.
lemma threshold_order : ∀ k : ℝ, 0 < k → ∀ t : ℝ,
    (coffee k t ≤ 60 ↔ Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / k ≤ t) := by
  intro k hk t
  calc
    coffee k t ≤ 60 ↔ Real.exp (-k * t) ≤ Real.exp (-Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ)) := by
      rw [threshold_exponential]
      unfold coffee
      constructor <;> intro h <;> linarith
    _ ↔ -k * t ≤ -Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) := Real.exp_le_exp
    _ ↔ Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / k ≤ t := by
      rw [div_le_iff₀ hk]
      constructor <;> intro h <;> nlinarith

/- Scientific answer, conditional on the family above:
(1) Every positive k fits 90 C initially, predicts a positive first hitting time
    log(7/4)/k, and is above 60 C before that time.
(2) For EVERY proposed positive time tau, some positive k gives exactly that
    first hitting time while fitting the same supplied initial temperature.
Thus these data cannot identify even a bounded positive cooling time. -/
theorem answer_theorem :
    (∀ k : ℝ, 0 < k →
      coffee k 0 = ((90 : ℕ) : ℝ) ∧
      0 < Real.log (((7 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((4 : ℕ) : ℝ) : ℝ) / k ∧
      (∀ t : ℝ, coffee k t = ((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) ↔ t = Real.log (((7 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((4 : ℕ) : ℝ) : ℝ) / k) ∧
      (∀ t : ℝ, coffee k t ≤ ((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) ↔ Real.log (((7 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((4 : ℕ) : ℝ) : ℝ) / k ≤ t)) ∧
    (∀ tau : ℝ, 0 < tau → ∃ k : ℝ, 0 < k ∧ coffee k 0 = ((90 : ℕ) : ℝ) ∧
      (∀ t : ℝ, coffee k t = ((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) ↔ t = tau) ∧
      (∀ t : ℝ, coffee k t ≤ ((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) ↔ tau ≤ t)) := by
  have hL : 0 < Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) := Real.log_pos (by norm_num)
  constructor
  · intro k hk
    exact ⟨initial_temperature k, div_pos hL hk, threshold_time k hk,
      threshold_order k hk⟩
  · intro tau htau
    let k := Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / tau
    have hk : 0 < k := div_pos hL htau
    have htime : Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / k = tau := by
      dsimp [k]
      field_simp
    refine ⟨k, hk, initial_temperature k, ?_, ?_⟩
    · intro t
      simpa only [Nat.cast_ofNat, htime] using (threshold_time k hk t)
    · intro t
      simpa only [Nat.cast_ofNat, htime] using (threshold_order k hk t)

checks/5efafd43ff7c4a5aa09d5aab1e165f16/submitted.lean
import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.Log.Basic
import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.ExpDeriv
import Mathlib.Tactic.NormNum
import Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith
import Mathlib.Tactic.FieldSimp
import Mathlib.Tactic.Ring
import Mathlib.Tactic.Convert

/- Conditional lumped heat-balance model. C is total heat capacity (J/K),
G total heat-loss conductance (W/K), t minutes, coffee temperature Celsius.
Ambient 20 C is chosen; initial 90 C is supplied. The 60 converts minutes
into seconds. Constant C and G, uniform temperature and initially hot mug
are modeling assumptions, not observations. -/
noncomputable def coffee (C G t : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  20 + (90 - 20) * Real.exp (-(60 * G / C) * t)

lemma initial_temperature : ∀ C G : ℝ, coffee C G 0 = 90 := by
  intro C G
  norm_num [coffee]

-- The chosen temperature family satisfies C dT/dt = -60 G (T-20).
lemma heat_balance : ∀ C G t : ℝ, 0 < C →
    HasDerivAt (coffee C G) (-(60 * G / C) * (coffee C G t - 20)) t ∧
    C * (-(60 * G / C) * (coffee C G t - 20)) =
      -60 * G * (coffee C G t - 20) := by
  intro C G t hC
  constructor
  · have h := (((hasDerivAt_id t).const_mul (-(60 * G / C))).exp.const_mul
      (90 - 20)).const_add 20
    change HasDerivAt (coffee C G)
      ((90 - 20) * (Real.exp (-(60 * G / C) * t) * (-(60 * G / C) * 1))) t at h
    convert h using 1 <;> dsimp [coffee] <;> ring
  · field_simp
    <;> ring

lemma threshold_exponential :
    Real.exp (-Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ)) = (4 / 7 : ℝ) := by
  have h : Real.log (4 / 7 : ℝ) = -Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) := by
    rw [← Real.log_inv]
    norm_num
  rw [← h, Real.exp_log (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 4 / 7)]

-- Solves the threshold equation and proves it is the first downward crossing.
lemma first_crossing : ∀ C G : ℝ, 0 < C → 0 < G →
    (∀ t : ℝ, coffee C G t = 60 ↔
      t = C * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G)) ∧
    (∀ t : ℝ, coffee C G t ≤ 60 ↔
      C * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G) ≤ t) := by
  intro C G hC hG
  have hk : 0 < 60 * G / C := div_pos (by positivity) hC
  have htime : Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G / C) =
      C * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G) := by field_simp
  constructor
  · intro t
    constructor
    · intro h
      have he : Real.exp (-(60 * G / C) * t) = (4 / 7 : ℝ) := by
        dsimp [coffee] at h
        linarith
      have hx := Real.exp_injective (he.trans threshold_exponential.symm)
      rw [← htime]
      apply (eq_div_iff (ne_of_gt hk)).2
      linarith
    · intro h
      rw [← htime] at h
      rw [h]
      have hx : -(60 * G / C) * (Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G / C)) =
          -Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) := by field_simp
      unfold coffee
      rw [hx, threshold_exponential]
      norm_num
  · intro t
    rw [← htime]
    calc
      coffee C G t ≤ 60 ↔
        Real.exp (-(60 * G / C) * t) ≤ Real.exp (-Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ)) := by
          rw [threshold_exponential]
          unfold coffee
          constructor <;> intro h <;> linarith
      _ ↔ -(60 * G / C) * t ≤ -Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) := Real.exp_le_exp
      _ ↔ Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G / C) ≤ t := by
        rw [div_le_iff₀ hk]
        constructor <;> intro h <;> linarith

-- Certified logarithm bounds, derived from log x <= x-1 and monotonicity.
-- No decimal logarithm or numerical cooling time is assumed.
lemma logarithm_bounds : (55 / 100 : ℝ) < Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) ∧
    Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) < (57 / 100 : ℝ) := by
  have hu := Real.log_lt_log (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 7 / 4)
    (by norm_num : (7 / 4 : ℝ) < (1019 / 1000 : ℝ) ^ 30)
  have hu' := Real.log_le_sub_one_of_pos (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 1019 / 1000)
  rw [Real.log_pow] at hu
  have hl := Real.log_lt_log (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 4 / 7)
    (by norm_num : (4 / 7 : ℝ) < (589 / 600 : ℝ) ^ 30)
  have hl' := Real.log_le_sub_one_of_pos (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 589 / 600)
  rw [Real.log_pow] at hl
  have hi : Real.log (4 / 7 : ℝ) = -Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) := by
    rw [← Real.log_inv]
    norm_num
  rw [hi] at hl
  constructor <;> linarith

/- Scientific uncertainty result. Rounded exposed side area 1/80 m^2 and
open surface area 1/200 m^2 are chosen for a ~250 mL cylinder, r~4 cm,
fill depth 5 cm. hs includes side convection+radiation+wall resistance;
ht includes top convection+radiation+evaporation. Base is insulated.
Assumed scenario ranges: C 1100..1300 J/K, hs 8..16, ht 20..70 W/m^2/K.
Conclusion: in every such constant-coefficient model, first 60 C crossing
is strictly between 18 and 62 minutes. These are NOT empirical bounds. -/
theorem scenario_answer :
    ∀ C hs ht : ℝ,
    ((1100 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ C → C ≤ ((1300 : ℕ) : ℝ) →
    ((8 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ hs → hs ≤ ((16 : ℕ) : ℝ) →
    ((20 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ ht → ht ≤ ((70 : ℕ) : ℝ) →
    ∃ tau : ℝ, ((18 : ℕ) : ℝ) < tau ∧ tau < ((62 : ℕ) : ℝ) ∧
      coffee C (hs / ((80 : ℕ) : ℝ) + ht / ((200 : ℕ) : ℝ)) 0 = ((90 : ℕ) : ℝ) ∧
      (∀ t : ℝ, coffee C (hs / ((80 : ℕ) : ℝ) + ht / ((200 : ℕ) : ℝ)) t =
        ((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) ↔ t = tau) ∧
      (∀ t : ℝ, coffee C (hs / ((80 : ℕ) : ℝ) + ht / ((200 : ℕ) : ℝ)) t ≤
        ((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) ↔ tau ≤ t) := by
  intro C hs ht hClo hChi hslo hshi htlo hthi
  norm_num at hClo hChi hslo hshi htlo hthi
  let G := hs / 80 + ht / 200
  have hGlo : (1 / 5 : ℝ) ≤ G := by dsimp [G]; linarith
  have hGhi : G ≤ (11 / 20 : ℝ) := by dsimp [G]; linarith
  have hC : 0 < C := by linarith
  have hG : 0 < G := by linarith
  have hden : 0 < 60 * G := by positivity
  obtain ⟨hLlo, hLhi⟩ := logarithm_bounds
  let tau := C * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G)
  have hlo : 18 < tau := by
    dsimp [tau]
    rw [lt_div_iff₀ hden]
    have hprod : C * (55 / 100) < C * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) :=
      mul_lt_mul_of_pos_left hLlo hC
    nlinarith
  have hhi : tau < 62 := by
    dsimp [tau]
    rw [div_lt_iff₀ hden]
    have hprod : C * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) < C * (57 / 100) :=
      mul_lt_mul_of_pos_left hLhi hC
    nlinarith
  exact ⟨tau, hlo, hhi, initial_temperature C G,
    (first_crossing C G hC hG).1, (first_crossing C G hC hG).2⟩

/- Worked nominal scenario: capacity = 0.25 kg *4200 J/kg/K water-like
coffee + 0.20 kg *800 J/kg/K chosen ceramic. hs=14, ht=35 W/m^2/K.
All these physical parameters are scenario assumptions, not fitted data.
Conclusion: first crossing is between 31 and 33 minutes. -/
theorem nominal_answer :

          (((14 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((80 : ℕ) : ℝ) + ((35 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((200 : ℕ) : ℝ)) t =
          ((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) ↔ t = tau) ∧
      (∀ t : ℝ,
        coffee ((1 / ((4 : ℕ) : ℝ)) * ((4200 : ℕ) : ℝ) +
          (1 / ((5 : ℕ) : ℝ)) * ((800 : ℕ) : ℝ))
          (((14 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((80 : ℕ) : ℝ) + ((35 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((200 : ℕ) : ℝ)) t ≤
          ((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) ↔ tau ≤ t) := by
  have hC : (0 : ℝ) < 1210 := by norm_num
  have hG : (0 : ℝ) < 7 / 20 := by norm_num
  have hcross := first_crossing 1210 (7 / 20) hC hG
  obtain ⟨hlo, hhi⟩ := logarithm_bounds
  refine ⟨1210 * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / 21, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · nlinarith
  · nlinarith
  · norm_num at hcross ⊢
    exact hcross.1
  · norm_num at hcross ⊢
    exact hcross.2

import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.Log.Basic
import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.ExpDeriv

import Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith
import Mathlib.Tactic.FieldSimp
import Mathlib.Tactic.Ring
import Mathlib.Tactic.Convert
import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.Trigonometric.Basic

/- Conditional lumped heat-balance model. C is total heat capacity (J/K),
G total heat-loss conductance (W/K), t minutes, coffee temperature Celsius.
Ambient 20 C is chosen; initial 90 C is supplied. The 60 converts minutes
into seconds. Constant C and G, uniform temperature and initially hot mug
are modeling assumptions, not observations. -/
noncomputable def coffee (C G t : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  20 + (90 - 20) * Real.exp (-(60 * G / C) * t)

lemma initial_temperature : ∀ C G : ℝ, coffee C G 0 = 90 := by
  intro C G
  norm_num [coffee]

-- The chosen temperature family satisfies C dT/dt = -60 G (T-20).
lemma heat_balance : ∀ C G t : ℝ, 0 < C →
    HasDerivAt (coffee C G) (-(60 * G / C) * (coffee C G t - 20)) t ∧
    C * (-(60 * G / C) * (coffee C G t - 20)) =
      -60 * G * (coffee C G t - 20) := by
  intro C G t hC
  constructor
  · have h := (((hasDerivAt_id t).const_mul (-(60 * G / C))).exp.const_mul
      (90 - 20)).const_add 20
    change HasDerivAt (coffee C G)
      ((90 - 20) * (Real.exp (-(60 * G / C) * t) * (-(60 * G / C) * 1))) t at h
    convert h using 1 <;> dsimp [coffee] <;> ring
  · field_simp
    <;> ring

lemma threshold_exponential :
    Real.exp (-Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ)) = (4 / 7 : ℝ) := by
  have h : Real.log (4 / 7 : ℝ) = -Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) := by
    rw [← Real.log_inv]
    norm_num
  rw [← h, Real.exp_log (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 4 / 7)]

-- Solves the threshold equation and proves it is the first downward crossing.
lemma first_crossing : ∀ C G : ℝ, 0 < C → 0 < G →
    (∀ t : ℝ, coffee C G t = 60 ↔
      t = C * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G)) ∧
    (∀ t : ℝ, coffee C G t ≤ 60 ↔
      C * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G) ≤ t) := by
  intro C G hC hG
  have hk : 0 < 60 * G / C := div_pos (by positivity) hC
  have htime : Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G / C) =
      C * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G) := by field_simp
  constructor
  · intro t
    constructor
    · intro h
      have he : Real.exp (-(60 * G / C) * t) = (4 / 7 : ℝ) := by
        dsimp [coffee] at h
        linarith
      have hx := Real.exp_injective (he.trans threshold_exponential.symm)
      rw [← htime]
      apply (eq_div_iff (ne_of_gt hk)).2
      linarith
    · intro h
      rw [← htime] at h
      rw [h]
      have hx : -(60 * G / C) * (Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G / C)) =
          -Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) := by field_simp
      unfold coffee
      rw [hx, threshold_exponential]
      norm_num
  · intro t
    rw [← htime]
    calc
      coffee C G t ≤ 60 ↔
        Real.exp (-(60 * G / C) * t) ≤ Real.exp (-Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ)) := by
          rw [threshold_exponential]
          unfold coffee
          constructor <;> intro h <;> linarith
      _ ↔ -(60 * G / C) * t ≤ -Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) := Real.exp_le_exp
      _ ↔ Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G / C) ≤ t := by
        rw [div_le_iff₀ hk]
        constructor <;> intro h <;> linarith

-- Certified logarithm bounds, derived from log x <= x-1 and monotonicity.
-- No decimal logarithm or numerical cooling time is assumed.
lemma logarithm_bounds : (55 / 100 : ℝ) < Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) ∧
    Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) < (57 / 100 : ℝ) := by
  have hu := Real.log_lt_log (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 7 / 4)
    (by norm_num : (7 / 4 : ℝ) < (1019 / 1000 : ℝ) ^ 30)
  have hu' := Real.log_le_sub_one_of_pos (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 1019 / 1000)
  rw [Real.log_pow] at hu
  have hl := Real.log_lt_log (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 4 / 7)
    (by norm_num : (4 / 7 : ℝ) < (589 / 600 : ℝ) ^ 30)
  have hl' := Real.log_le_sub_one_of_pos (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 589 / 600)
  rw [Real.log_pow] at hl
  have hi : Real.log (4 / 7 : ℝ) = -Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) := by
    rw [← Real.log_inv]
    norm_num
  rw [hi] at hl
  constructor <;> linarith

/- Chosen dimensions in metres: internal radius .04, wall .004,
liquid and exposed side height .05. Only the side adjacent to liquid loses
heat in this approximation; rim, handle, base and wall above liquid are neglected.
Areas are exact cylinder formulae, not independently assigned rounded values. -/
noncomputable def sideArea : ℝ := 2 * Real.pi * (4 / 100 + 4 / 1000) * (5 / 100)
noncomputable def topArea : ℝ := Real.pi * (4 / 100) ^ 2

-- Combined thermal storage: masses kg, specific heats J/(kg K), result J/K.
def heatCapacity (mc cc mm cm : ℝ) : ℝ := mc * cc + mm * cm

-- Derived capacity bounds, not additional assumptions about total C.
lemma capacity_bounds : ∀ mc cc mm cm : ℝ,
    24 / 100 ≤ mc → mc ≤ 26 / 100 →
    4180 ≤ cc → cc ≤ 4220 →
    18 / 100 ≤ mm → mm ≤ 22 / 100 →
    750 ≤ cm → cm ≤ 900 →
    11382 / 10 ≤ heatCapacity mc cc mm cm ∧
    heatCapacity mc cc mm cm ≤ 12952 / 10 := by
  intro mc cc mm cm hmcl hmcu hccl hccu hmml hmmu hcml hcmu
  have hmc : 0 ≤ mc := by linarith
  have hmm : 0 ≤ mm := by linarith
  have hlc := mul_le_mul hmcl hccl (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 4180) hmc
  have hlm := mul_le_mul hmml hcml (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 750) hmm
  have huc := mul_le_mul hmcu hccu (by linarith : 0 ≤ cc)
    (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 26 / 100)
  have hum := mul_le_mul hmmu hcmu (by linarith : 0 ≤ cm)
    (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 22 / 100)
  dsimp [heatCapacity]
  constructor <;> nlinarith

-- Technical square-root enclosures used only to certify geometric arithmetic.
lemma sqrt_enclosure : ∀ x a b : ℝ, 0 ≤ a → 0 ≤ b → a^2 ≤ x → x ≤ b^2 →
    a ≤ Real.sqrt x ∧ Real.sqrt x ≤ b := by
  intro x a b ha hb hl hu
  have hx : 0 ≤ x := by nlinarith [sq_nonneg a]
  have hs := Real.sq_sqrt hx
  have hn := Real.sqrt_nonneg x
  constructor <;> nlinarith

-- Pi bounds are proved from the installed trigonometric Taylor remainder,
-- not supplied as numerical assumptions.
lemma pi_bounds : (314 / 100 : ℝ) < Real.pi ∧ Real.pi < (3142 / 1000 : ℝ) := by
  obtain ⟨hl0, hu0⟩ := sqrt_enclosure (2 : ℝ) (14142135623 / 10000000000) (14142135624 / 10000000000) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num)
  obtain ⟨hl1, hu1⟩ := sqrt_enclosure (2 + Real.sqrt 2) (18477590650 / 10000000000) (18477590651 / 10000000000) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num; linarith [hl0, hu0]) (by norm_num; linarith [hl0, hu0])
  obtain ⟨hl2, hu2⟩ := sqrt_enclosure (2 + Real.sqrt (2 + Real.sqrt 2)) (19615705608 / 10000000000) (19615705609 / 10000000000) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num; linarith [hl1, hu1]) (by norm_num; linarith [hl1, hu1])
  obtain ⟨hl3, hu3⟩ := sqrt_enclosure (2 - Real.sqrt (2 + Real.sqrt (2 + Real.sqrt 2))) (1960342700 / 10000000000) (1960343000 / 10000000000) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num; linarith [hl2, hu2]) (by norm_num; linarith [hl2, hu2])
  have hs : (9801713 / 100000000 : ℝ) ≤ Real.sin (Real.pi / 32) ∧
      Real.sin (Real.pi / 32) ≤ (9801715 / 100000000 : ℝ) := by
    rw [Real.sin_pi_div_thirty_two]
    constructor <;> linarith
  have hp := Real.pi_pos
  have hp4 := Real.pi_le_four
  have hx : |Real.pi / 32| ≤ 1 := by rw [abs_of_pos (by positivity)]; linarith
  have he := Real.sin_bound hx
  rw [abs_of_pos (by positivity : 0 < Real.pi / 32)] at he
  obtain ⟨hel, heu⟩ := abs_le.mp he
  have hsmall : 0 ≤ Real.pi / 32 ∧ Real.pi / 32 ≤ 1 / 8 := by constructor <;> linarith
  have hcubic : (Real.pi / 32)^3 ≤ (1 / 8 : ℝ)^3 := pow_le_pow_left₀ hsmall.1 hsmall.2 3
  have hfifth : (Real.pi / 32)^5 ≤ (1 / 8 : ℝ)^5 := pow_le_pow_left₀ hsmall.1 hsmall.2 5
  have hp315 : Real.pi < 315 / 100 := by nlinarith [hs.2]
  have hxhi : Real.pi / 32 ≤ (1 / 10 : ℝ) := by linarith
  have hcubic2 : (Real.pi / 32)^3 ≤ (1 / 10 : ℝ)^3 := pow_le_pow_left₀ hsmall.1 hxhi 3
  have hfifth2 : (Real.pi / 32)^5 ≤ (1 / 10 : ℝ)^5 := pow_le_pow_left₀ hsmall.1 hxhi 5
  have hxlo : (98 / 1000 : ℝ) ≤ Real.pi / 32 := by nlinarith [hs.1, pow_nonneg hsmall.1 3]
  have hcubiclo : (98 / 1000 : ℝ)^3 ≤ (Real.pi / 32)^3 :=
    pow_le_pow_left₀ (by norm_num) hxlo 3
  constructor <;> nlinarith [hs.1, hs.2]

-- Rounded enclosures are proved to contain the exact cylindrical areas.
lemma area_bounds :
    (138 / 10000 : ℝ) ≤ sideArea ∧ sideArea ≤ (1383 / 100000 : ℝ) ∧
    (502 / 100000 : ℝ) ≤ topArea ∧ topArea ≤ (503 / 100000 : ℝ) := by
  obtain ⟨hlo, hhi⟩ := pi_bounds
  dsimp [sideArea, topArea]
  norm_num at hlo hhi ⊢
  constructor
  · linarith
  constructor
  · linarith
  constructor <;> linarith

/- Conditional uncertainty result: all masses, specific heats and coefficients
below are explicit scenario assumptions. Combined C and G bounds are derived.
Uniform preheated coffee+mug, ambient 20 C, constant effective coefficients.
The unique first 60 C crossing is between 18 and 59 minutes. -/
theorem scenario_answer :
    ∀ mc cc mm cm hs ht : ℝ,
    ((24 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((100 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ mc → mc ≤ ((26 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((100 : ℕ) : ℝ) →
    ((4180 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ cc → cc ≤ ((4220 : ℕ) : ℝ) →
    ((18 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((100 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ mm → mm ≤ ((22 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((100 : ℕ) : ℝ) →
    ((750 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ cm → cm ≤ ((900 : ℕ) : ℝ) →
    ((8 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ hs → hs ≤ ((16 : ℕ) : ℝ) → ((20 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ ht → ht ≤ ((70 : ℕ) : ℝ) →
    ∃ tau : ℝ, ((18 : ℕ) : ℝ) < tau ∧ tau < ((59 : ℕ) : ℝ) ∧
      coffee (heatCapacity mc cc mm cm) (hs * sideArea + ht * topArea) ((0 : ℕ) : ℝ) = ((90 : ℕ) : ℝ) ∧
      (∀ t : ℝ, coffee (heatCapacity mc cc mm cm)
        (hs * sideArea + ht * topArea) t = ((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) ↔ t = tau) ∧
      (∀ t : ℝ, coffee (heatCapacity mc cc mm cm)
        (hs * sideArea + ht * topArea) t ≤ ((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) ↔ tau ≤ t) := by
  intro mc cc mm cm hs ht hmcl hmcu hccl hccu hmml hmmu hcml hcmu hslo hshi htlo hthi
  norm_num only [Nat.cast_ofNat] at hmcl hmcu hccl hccu hmml hmmu hcml hcmu hslo hshi htlo hthi ⊢
  obtain ⟨hClo, hChi⟩ := capacity_bounds mc cc mm cm (by linarith) (by linarith) hccl hccu (by linarith) (by linarith) hcml hcmu
  obtain ⟨hAslo, hAshi, hAtlo, hAthi⟩ := area_bounds
  let C := heatCapacity mc cc mm cm
  let G := hs * sideArea + ht * topArea
  have hAs : 0 ≤ sideArea := by linarith
  have hAt : 0 ≤ topArea := by linarith
  have hsl := mul_le_mul hslo hAslo (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 138 / 10000)
    (by linarith : 0 ≤ hs)
  have htl := mul_le_mul htlo hAtlo (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 502 / 100000)
    (by linarith : 0 ≤ ht)
  have hsu := mul_le_mul hshi hAshi hAs (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 16)
  have htu := mul_le_mul hthi hAthi hAt (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 70)
  have hGlo : (2108 / 10000 : ℝ) ≤ G := by dsimp [G]; linarith
  have hGhi : G ≤ (57338 / 100000 : ℝ) := by dsimp [G]; linarith
  have hC : 0 < C := by dsimp [C]; linarith
  have hG : 0 < G := by linarith
  have hden : 0 < 60 * G := by positivity
  obtain ⟨hLlo, hLhi⟩ := logarithm_bounds
  let tau := C * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G)
  have hlo : 18 < tau := by
    dsimp [tau]
    rw [lt_div_iff₀ hden]
    have hprod := mul_lt_mul_of_pos_left hLlo hC
    change 11382 / 10 ≤ C at hClo
    nlinarith
  have hhi : tau < 59 := by
    dsimp [tau]
    rw [div_lt_iff₀ hden]
    have hprod := mul_lt_mul_of_pos_left hLhi hC
    change C ≤ 12952 / 10 at hChi
    nlinarith
  exact ⟨tau, hlo, hhi, initial_temperature C G,
    (first_crossing C G hC hG).1, (first_crossing C G hC hG).2⟩

/- Nominal masses .25/.20 kg, specific heats 4200/800 J/(kg K),
side/top coefficients 14/35 W/(m^2 K), exact cylindrical areas above.
These are scenario values, not measurements. First crossing: 30..32 minutes. -/
theorem nominal_answer :
    ∃ tau : ℝ, ((30 : ℕ) : ℝ) < tau ∧ tau < ((32 : ℕ) : ℝ) ∧
      (∀ t : ℝ, coffee (heatCapacity (((1 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((4 : ℕ) : ℝ)) ((4200 : ℕ) : ℝ) (((1 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((5 : ℕ) : ℝ)) ((800 : ℕ) : ℝ))
        (((14 : ℕ) : ℝ) * sideArea + ((35 : ℕ) : ℝ) * topArea) t = ((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) ↔ t = tau) ∧
      (∀ t : ℝ, coffee (heatCapacity (((1 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((4 : ℕ) : ℝ)) ((4200 : ℕ) : ℝ) (((1 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((5 : ℕ) : ℝ)) ((800 : ℕ) : ℝ))
        (((14 : ℕ) : ℝ) * sideArea + ((35 : ℕ) : ℝ) * topArea) t ≤ ((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) ↔ tau ≤ t) := by
  norm_num only [Nat.cast_ofNat]
  obtain ⟨hAslo, hAshi, hAtlo, hAthi⟩ := area_bounds
  let G := 14 * sideArea + 35 * topArea
  have hGlo : (3689 / 10000 : ℝ) ≤ G := by dsimp [G]; linarith
  have hGhi : G ≤ (36967 / 100000 : ℝ) := by dsimp [G]; linarith
  have hG : 0 < G := by linarith
  have hden : 0 < 60 * G := by positivity
  have hcross := first_crossing 1210 G (by norm_num) hG
  obtain ⟨hlo, hhi⟩ := logarithm_bounds
  refine ⟨1210 * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G), ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · rw [lt_div_iff₀ hden]
    nlinarith
  · rw [div_lt_iff₀ hden]
    nlinarith
  · norm_num [heatCapacity] at hcross ⊢
    exact hcross.1
  · norm_num [heatCapacity] at hcross ⊢
    exact hcross.2

import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.Log.Basic
import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.ExpDeriv
import Mathlib.Tactic.NormNum
import Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith
import Mathlib.Tactic.FieldSimp
import Mathlib.Tactic.Ring
import Mathlib.Tactic.Convert
import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.Trigonometric.Basic

/- Conditional lumped heat-balance model. C is total heat capacity (J/K),
G total heat-loss conductance (W/K), t minutes, coffee temperature Celsius.
Ambient 20 C is chosen; initial 90 C is supplied. The 60 converts minutes
into seconds. Constant C and G, uniform temperature and initially hot mug
are modeling assumptions, not observations. -/
noncomputable def coffee (C G t : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  20 + (90 - 20) * Real.exp (-(60 * G / C) * t)

lemma initial_temperature : ∀ C G : ℝ, coffee C G 0 = 90 := by
  intro C G
  norm_num [coffee]

-- The chosen temperature family satisfies C dT/dt = -60 G (T-20).
lemma heat_balance : ∀ C G t : ℝ, 0 < C →
    HasDerivAt (coffee C G) (-(60 * G / C) * (coffee C G t - 20)) t ∧
    C * (-(60 * G / C) * (coffee C G t - 20)) =
      -60 * G * (coffee C G t - 20) := by
  intro C G t hC
  constructor
  · have h := (((hasDerivAt_id t).const_mul (-(60 * G / C))).exp.const_mul
      (90 - 20)).const_add 20
    change HasDerivAt (coffee C G)
      ((90 - 20) * (Real.exp (-(60 * G / C) * t) * (-(60 * G / C) * 1))) t at h
    convert h using 1 <;> dsimp [coffee] <;> ring
  · field_simp
    <;> ring

lemma threshold_exponential :
    Real.exp (-Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ)) = (4 / 7 : ℝ) := by
  have h : Real.log (4 / 7 : ℝ) = -Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) := by
    rw [← Real.log_inv]
    norm_num
  rw [← h, Real.exp_log (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 4 / 7)]

-- Solves the threshold equation and proves it is the first downward crossing.
lemma first_crossing : ∀ C G : ℝ, 0 < C → 0 < G →
    (∀ t : ℝ, coffee C G t = 60 ↔
      t = C * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G)) ∧
    (∀ t : ℝ, coffee C G t ≤ 60 ↔
      C * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G) ≤ t) := by
  intro C G hC hG
  have hk : 0 < 60 * G / C := div_pos (by positivity) hC
  have htime : Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G / C) =
      C * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G) := by field_simp
  constructor
  · intro t
    constructor
    · intro h
      have he : Real.exp (-(60 * G / C) * t) = (4 / 7 : ℝ) := by
        dsimp [coffee] at h
        linarith
      have hx := Real.exp_injective (he.trans threshold_exponential.symm)
      rw [← htime]
      apply (eq_div_iff (ne_of_gt hk)).2
      linarith
    · intro h
      rw [← htime] at h
      rw [h]
      have hx : -(60 * G / C) * (Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G / C)) =
          -Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) := by field_simp
      unfold coffee
      rw [hx, threshold_exponential]
      norm_num
  · intro t
    rw [← htime]
    calc
      coffee C G t ≤ 60 ↔
        Real.exp (-(60 * G / C) * t) ≤ Real.exp (-Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ)) := by
          rw [threshold_exponential]
          unfold coffee
          constructor <;> intro h <;> linarith
      _ ↔ -(60 * G / C) * t ≤ -Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) := Real.exp_le_exp
      _ ↔ Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G / C) ≤ t := by
        rw [div_le_iff₀ hk]
        constructor <;> intro h <;> linarith

  obtain ⟨hl2, hu2⟩ := sqrt_enclosure (2 + Real.sqrt (2 + Real.sqrt 2)) (19615705608 / 10000000000) (19615705609 / 10000000000) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num; linarith [hl1, hu1]) (by norm_num; linarith [hl1, hu1])
  obtain ⟨hl3, hu3⟩ := sqrt_enclosure (2 - Real.sqrt (2 + Real.sqrt (2 + Real.sqrt 2))) (1960342700 / 10000000000) (1960343000 / 10000000000) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num; linarith [hl2, hu2]) (by norm_num; linarith [hl2, hu2])
  have hs : (9801713 / 100000000 : ℝ) ≤ Real.sin (Real.pi / 32) ∧
      Real.sin (Real.pi / 32) ≤ (9801715 / 100000000 : ℝ) := by
    rw [Real.sin_pi_div_thirty_two]
    constructor <;> linarith
  have hp := Real.pi_pos
  have hp4 := Real.pi_le_four
  have hx : |Real.pi / 32| ≤ 1 := by rw [abs_of_pos (by positivity)]; linarith
  have he := Real.sin_bound hx
  rw [abs_of_pos (by positivity : 0 < Real.pi / 32)] at he
  obtain ⟨hel, heu⟩ := abs_le.mp he
  have hsmall : 0 ≤ Real.pi / 32 ∧ Real.pi / 32 ≤ 1 / 8 := by constructor <;> linarith
  have hcubic : (Real.pi / 32)^3 ≤ (1 / 8 : ℝ)^3 := pow_le_pow_left₀ hsmall.1 hsmall.2 3
  have hfifth : (Real.pi / 32)^5 ≤ (1 / 8 : ℝ)^5 := pow_le_pow_left₀ hsmall.1 hsmall.2 5
  have hp315 : Real.pi < 315 / 100 := by nlinarith [hs.2]
  have hxhi : Real.pi / 32 ≤ (1 / 10 : ℝ) := by linarith
  have hcubic2 : (Real.pi / 32)^3 ≤ (1 / 10 : ℝ)^3 := pow_le_pow_left₀ hsmall.1 hxhi 3
  have hfifth2 : (Real.pi / 32)^5 ≤ (1 / 10 : ℝ)^5 := pow_le_pow_left₀ hsmall.1 hxhi 5
  have hxlo : (98 / 1000 : ℝ) ≤ Real.pi / 32 := by nlinarith [hs.1, pow_nonneg hsmall.1 3]
  have hcubiclo : (98 / 1000 : ℝ)^3 ≤ (Real.pi / 32)^3 :=
    pow_le_pow_left₀ (by norm_num) hxlo 3
  constructor <;> nlinarith [hs.1, hs.2]

-- Rounded enclosures are proved to contain the exact cylindrical areas.
lemma area_bounds :
    (138 / 10000 : ℝ) ≤ sideArea ∧ sideArea ≤ (1383 / 100000 : ℝ) ∧
    (502 / 100000 : ℝ) ≤ topArea ∧ topArea ≤ (503 / 100000 : ℝ) := by
  obtain ⟨hlo, hhi⟩ := pi_bounds
  dsimp [sideArea, topArea]
  norm_num at hlo hhi ⊢
  constructor
  · linarith
  constructor
  · linarith
  constructor <;> linarith

/- Conditional uncertainty result: all masses, specific heats and coefficients
below are explicit scenario assumptions. Combined C and G bounds are derived.
Uniform preheated coffee+mug, ambient 20 C, constant effective coefficients.
The unique first 60 C crossing is between 18 and 59 minutes. -/
theorem scenario_answer :
    ∀ mc cc mm cm hs ht : ℝ,
    ((24 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((100 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ mc → mc ≤ ((26 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((100 : ℕ) : ℝ) →
    ((4180 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ cc → cc ≤ ((4220 : ℕ) : ℝ) →
    ((18 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((100 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ mm → mm ≤ ((22 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((100 : ℕ) : ℝ) →
    ((750 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ cm → cm ≤ ((900 : ℕ) : ℝ) →
    ((8 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ hs → hs ≤ ((16 : ℕ) : ℝ) → ((20 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ ht → ht ≤ ((70 : ℕ) : ℝ) →
    ∃ tau : ℝ, ((18 : ℕ) : ℝ) < tau ∧ tau < ((59 : ℕ) : ℝ) ∧
      coffee (heatCapacity mc cc mm cm) (hs * sideArea + ht * topArea) ((0 : ℕ) : ℝ) = ((90 : ℕ) : ℝ) ∧
      (∀ t : ℝ, coffee (heatCapacity mc cc mm cm)
        (hs * sideArea + ht * topArea) t = ((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) ↔ t = tau) ∧
      (∀ t : ℝ, coffee (heatCapacity mc cc mm cm)
        (hs * sideArea + ht * topArea) t ≤ ((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) ↔ tau ≤ t) := by
  intro mc cc mm cm hs ht hmcl hmcu hccl hccu hmml hmmu hcml hcmu hslo hshi htlo hthi
  norm_num at hmcl hmcu hccl hccu hmml hmmu hcml hcmu hslo hshi htlo hthi ⊢
  obtain ⟨hClo, hChi⟩ := capacity_bounds mc cc mm cm hmcl hmcu hccl hccu hmml hmmu hcml hcmu
  obtain ⟨hAslo, hAshi, hAtlo, hAthi⟩ := area_bounds
  let C := heatCapacity mc cc mm cm
  let G := hs * sideArea + ht * topArea
  have hAs : 0 ≤ sideArea := by linarith
  have hAt : 0 ≤ topArea := by linarith
  have hsl := mul_le_mul hslo hAslo (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 138 / 10000)
    (by linarith : 0 ≤ hs)
  have htl := mul_le_mul htlo hAtlo (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 502 / 100000)
    (by linarith : 0 ≤ ht)
  have hsu := mul_le_mul hshi hAshi hAs (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 16)
  have htu := mul_le_mul hthi hAthi hAt (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 70)
  have hGlo : (2108 / 10000 : ℝ) ≤ G := by dsimp [G]; linarith
  have hGhi : G ≤ (57338 / 100000 : ℝ) := by dsimp [G]; linarith
  have hC : 0 < C := by dsimp [C]; linarith
  have hG : 0 < G := by linarith
  have hden : 0 < 60 * G := by positivity
  obtain ⟨hLlo, hLhi⟩ := logarithm_bounds
  let tau := C * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G)
  have hlo : 18 < tau := by
    dsimp [tau]
    rw [lt_div_iff₀ hden]
    have hprod := mul_lt_mul_of_pos_left hLlo hC
    change 11382 / 10 ≤ C at hClo
    nlinarith
  have hhi : tau < 59 := by
    dsimp [tau]
    rw [div_lt_iff₀ hden]
    have hprod := mul_lt_mul_of_pos_left hLhi hC
    change C ≤ 12952 / 10 at hChi
    nlinarith
  exact ⟨tau, hlo, hhi, initial_temperature C G,
    (first_crossing C G hC hG).1, (first_crossing C G hC hG).2⟩

/- Nominal masses .25/.20 kg, specific heats 4200/800 J/(kg K),
side/top coefficients 14/35 W/(m^2 K), exact cylindrical areas above.
These are scenario values, not measurements. First crossing: 30..32 minutes. -/
theorem nominal_answer :
    ∃ tau : ℝ, ((30 : ℕ) : ℝ) < tau ∧ tau < ((32 : ℕ) : ℝ) ∧
      (∀ t : ℝ, coffee (heatCapacity (((1 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((4 : ℕ) : ℝ)) ((4200 : ℕ) : ℝ) (((1 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((5 : ℕ) : ℝ)) ((800 : ℕ) : ℝ))
        (((14 : ℕ) : ℝ) * sideArea + ((35 : ℕ) : ℝ) * topArea) t = ((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) ↔ t = tau) ∧
      (∀ t : ℝ, coffee (heatCapacity (((1 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((4 : ℕ) : ℝ)) ((4200 : ℕ) : ℝ) (((1 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((5 : ℕ) : ℝ)) ((800 : ℕ) : ℝ))
        (((14 : ℕ) : ℝ) * sideArea + ((35 : ℕ) : ℝ) * topArea) t ≤ ((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) ↔ tau ≤ t) := by
  norm_num only [Nat.cast_ofNat]
  obtain ⟨hAslo, hAshi, hAtlo, hAthi⟩ := area_bounds
  let G := 14 * sideArea + 35 * topArea
  have hGlo : (3689 / 10000 : ℝ) ≤ G := by dsimp [G]; linarith
  have hGhi : G ≤ (36967 / 100000 : ℝ) := by dsimp [G]; linarith
  have hG : 0 < G := by linarith
  have hden : 0 < 60 * G := by positivity
  have hcross := first_crossing 1210 G (by norm_num) hG
  obtain ⟨hlo, hhi⟩ := logarithm_bounds
  refine ⟨1210 * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G), ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · rw [lt_div_iff₀ hden]
    nlinarith
  · rw [div_lt_iff₀ hden]
    nlinarith
  · norm_num [heatCapacity] at hcross ⊢
    exact hcross.1
  · norm_num [heatCapacity] at hcross ⊢
    exact hcross.2

import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.Log.Basic
import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.ExpDeriv
import Mathlib.Tactic.NormNum
import Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith
import Mathlib.Tactic.FieldSimp
import Mathlib.Tactic.Ring
import Mathlib.Tactic.Convert
import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.Trigonometric.Basic

/- Conditional lumped heat-balance model. C is total heat capacity (J/K),
G total heat-loss conductance (W/K), t minutes, coffee temperature Celsius.
Ambient 20 C is chosen; initial 90 C is supplied. The 60 converts minutes
into seconds. Constant C and G, uniform temperature and initially hot mug
are modeling assumptions, not observations. -/
noncomputable def coffee (C G t : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  20 + (90 - 20) * Real.exp (-(60 * G / C) * t)

lemma initial_temperature : ∀ C G : ℝ, coffee C G 0 = 90 := by
  intro C G
  norm_num [coffee]

-- The chosen temperature family satisfies C dT/dt = -60 G (T-20).
lemma heat_balance : ∀ C G t : ℝ, 0 < C →
    HasDerivAt (coffee C G) (-(60 * G / C) * (coffee C G t - 20)) t ∧
    C * (-(60 * G / C) * (coffee C G t - 20)) =
      -60 * G * (coffee C G t - 20) := by
  intro C G t hC
  constructor
  · have h := (((hasDerivAt_id t).const_mul (-(60 * G / C))).exp.const_mul
      (90 - 20)).const_add 20
    change HasDerivAt (coffee C G)
      ((90 - 20) * (Real.exp (-(60 * G / C) * t) * (-(60 * G / C) * 1))) t at h
    convert h using 1 <;> dsimp [coffee] <;> ring
  · field_simp
    <;> ring

lemma threshold_exponential :
    Real.exp (-Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ)) = (4 / 7 : ℝ) := by
  have h : Real.log (4 / 7 : ℝ) = -Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) := by
    rw [← Real.log_inv]
    norm_num
  rw [← h, Real.exp_log (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 4 / 7)]

-- Solves the threshold equation and proves it is the first downward crossing.
lemma first_crossing : ∀ C G : ℝ, 0 < C → 0 < G →
    (∀ t : ℝ, coffee C G t = 60 ↔
      t = C * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G)) ∧
    (∀ t : ℝ, coffee C G t ≤ 60 ↔
      C * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G) ≤ t) := by
  intro C G hC hG
  have hk : 0 < 60 * G / C := div_pos (by positivity) hC
  have htime : Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G / C) =
      C * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G) := by field_simp
  constructor
  · intro t
    constructor
    · intro h
      have he : Real.exp (-(60 * G / C) * t) = (4 / 7 : ℝ) := by
        dsimp [coffee] at h
        linarith
      have hx := Real.exp_injective (he.trans threshold_exponential.symm)
      rw [← htime]
      apply (eq_div_iff (ne_of_gt hk)).2
      linarith
    · intro h
      rw [← htime] at h
      rw [h]
      have hx : -(60 * G / C) * (Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G / C)) =
          -Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) := by field_simp
      unfold coffee
      rw [hx, threshold_exponential]
      norm_num
  · intro t
    rw [← htime]
    calc
      coffee C G t ≤ 60 ↔
        Real.exp (-(60 * G / C) * t) ≤ Real.exp (-Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ)) := by
          rw [threshold_exponential]
          unfold coffee
          constructor <;> intro h <;> linarith
      _ ↔ -(60 * G / C) * t ≤ -Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) := Real.exp_le_exp
      _ ↔ Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G / C) ≤ t := by
        rw [div_le_iff₀ hk]
        constructor <;> intro h <;> linarith

-- Certified logarithm bounds, derived from log x <= x-1 and monotonicity.
-- No decimal logarithm or numerical cooling time is assumed.
lemma logarithm_bounds : (55 / 100 : ℝ) < Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) ∧
    Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) < (57 / 100 : ℝ) := by
  have hu := Real.log_lt_log (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 7 / 4)
    (by norm_num : (7 / 4 : ℝ) < (1019 / 1000 : ℝ) ^ 30)
  have hu' := Real.log_le_sub_one_of_pos (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 1019 / 1000)
  rw [Real.log_pow] at hu
  have hl := Real.log_lt_log (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 4 / 7)
    (by norm_num : (4 / 7 : ℝ) < (589 / 600 : ℝ) ^ 30)
  have hl' := Real.log_le_sub_one_of_pos (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 589 / 600)
  rw [Real.log_pow] at hl
  have hi : Real.log (4 / 7 : ℝ) = -Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) := by
    rw [← Real.log_inv]
    norm_num
  rw [hi] at hl
  constructor <;> linarith

/- Chosen dimensions in metres: internal radius .04, wall .004,
liquid and exposed side height .05. Only the side adjacent to liquid loses
heat in this approximation; rim, handle, base and wall above liquid are neglected.
Areas are exact cylinder formulae, not independently assigned rounded values. -/
noncomputable def sideArea : ℝ := 2 * Real.pi * (4 / 100 + 4 / 1000) * (5 / 100)
noncomputable def topArea : ℝ := Real.pi * (4 / 100) ^ 2

-- Combined thermal storage: masses kg, specific heats J/(kg K), result J/K.
def heatCapacity (mc cc mm cm : ℝ) : ℝ := mc * cc + mm * cm

-- Derived capacity bounds, not additional assumptions about total C.
lemma capacity_bounds : ∀ mc cc mm cm : ℝ,
    24 / 100 ≤ mc → mc ≤ 26 / 100 →
    4180 ≤ cc → cc ≤ 4220 →
    18 / 100 ≤ mm → mm ≤ 22 / 100 →
    750 ≤ cm → cm ≤ 900 →
    11382 / 10 ≤ heatCapacity mc cc mm cm ∧
    heatCapacity mc cc mm cm ≤ 12952 / 10 := by
  intro mc cc mm cm hmcl hmcu hccl hccu hmml hmmu hcml hcmu
  have hmc : 0 ≤ mc := by linarith
  have hmm : 0 ≤ mm := by linarith
  have hlc := mul_le_mul hmcl hccl (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 4180) hmc
  have hlm := mul_le_mul hmml hcml (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 750) hmm
  have huc := mul_le_mul hmcu hccu (by linarith : 0 ≤ cc)
    (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 26 / 100)
  have hum := mul_le_mul hmmu hcmu (by linarith : 0 ≤ cm)
    (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 22 / 100)
  dsimp [heatCapacity]
  constructor <;> nlinarith

-- Technical square-root enclosures used only to certify geometric arithmetic.
lemma sqrt_enclosure : ∀ x a b : ℝ, 0 ≤ a → 0 ≤ b → a^2 ≤ x → x ≤ b^2 →
    a ≤ Real.sqrt x ∧ Real.sqrt x ≤ b := by
  intro x a b ha hb hl hu
  have hx : 0 ≤ x := by nlinarith [sq_nonneg a]
  have hs := Real.sq_sqrt hx
  have hn := Real.sqrt_nonneg x
  constructor <;> nlinarith

-- Pi bounds are proved from the installed trigonometric Taylor remainder,
-- not supplied as numerical assumptions.
lemma pi_bounds : (314 / 100 : ℝ) < Real.pi ∧ Real.pi < (3142 / 1000 : ℝ) := by
  obtain ⟨hl0, hu0⟩ := sqrt_enclosure (2 : ℝ) (14142135623 / 10000000000) (14142135624 / 10000000000) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num)
  obtain ⟨hl1, hu1⟩ := sqrt_enclosure (2 + Real.sqrt 2) (18477590650 / 10000000000) (18477590651 / 10000000000) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num; linarith [hl0, hu0]) (by norm_num; linarith [hl0, hu0])
  obtain ⟨hl2, hu2⟩ := sqrt_enclosure (2 + Real.sqrt (2 + Real.sqrt 2)) (19615705608 / 10000000000) (19615705609 / 10000000000) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num; linarith [hl1, hu1]) (by norm_num; linarith [hl1, hu1])
  obtain ⟨hl3, hu3⟩ := sqrt_enclosure (2 - Real.sqrt (2 + Real.sqrt (2 + Real.sqrt 2))) (1960342700 / 10000000000) (1960343000 / 10000000000) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num; linarith [hl2, hu2]) (by norm_num; linarith [hl2, hu2])
  have hs : (9801713 / 100000000 : ℝ) ≤ Real.sin (Real.pi / 32) ∧
      Real.sin (Real.pi / 32) ≤ (9801715 / 100000000 : ℝ) := by
    rw [Real.sin_pi_div_thirty_two]
    constructor <;> linarith
  have hp := Real.pi_pos
  have hp4 := Real.pi_le_four
  have hx : |Real.pi / 32| ≤ 1 := by rw [abs_of_pos (by positivity)]; linarith
  have he := Real.sin_bound hx
  rw [abs_of_pos (by positivity : 0 < Real.pi / 32)] at he
  obtain ⟨hel, heu⟩ := abs_le.mp he
  have hsmall : 0 ≤ Real.pi / 32 ∧ Real.pi / 32 ≤ 1 / 8 := by constructor <;> linarith
  have hcubic : (Real.pi / 32)^3 ≤ (1 / 8 : ℝ)^3 := pow_le_pow_left₀ hsmall.1 hsmall.2 3
  have hfifth : (Real.pi / 32)^5 ≤ (1 / 8 : ℝ)^5 := pow_le_pow_left₀ hsmall.1 hsmall.2 5
  have hp315 : Real.pi < 315 / 100 := by nlinarith [hs.2]
  have hxhi : Real.pi / 32 ≤ (1 / 10 : ℝ) := by linarith
  have hcubic2 : (Real.pi / 32)^3 ≤ (1 / 10 : ℝ)^3 := pow_le_pow_left₀ hsmall.1 hxhi 3
  have hfifth2 : (Real.pi / 32)^5 ≤ (1 / 10 : ℝ)^5 := pow_le_pow_left₀ hsmall.1 hxhi 5
  have hxlo : (98 / 1000 : ℝ) ≤ Real.pi / 32 := by nlinarith [hs.1, pow_nonneg hsmall.1 3]
  have hcubiclo : (98 / 1000 : ℝ)^3 ≤ (Real.pi / 32)^3 :=
    pow_le_pow_left₀ (by norm_num) hxlo 3
  constructor <;> nlinarith [hs.1, hs.2]

-- Rounded enclosures are proved to contain the exact cylindrical areas.
lemma area_bounds :
    (138 / 10000 : ℝ) ≤ sideArea ∧ sideArea ≤ (1383 / 100000 : ℝ) ∧
    (502 / 100000 : ℝ) ≤ topArea ∧ topArea ≤ (503 / 100000 : ℝ) := by
  obtain ⟨hlo, hhi⟩ := pi_bounds
  dsimp [sideArea, topArea]
  norm_num at hlo hhi ⊢
  constructor
  · linarith
  constructor
  · linarith
  constructor <;> linarith

/- Conditional uncertainty result: all masses, specific heats and coefficients
below are explicit scenario assumptions. Combined C and G bounds are derived.
Uniform preheated coffee+mug, ambient 20 C, constant effective coefficients.
The unique first 60 C crossing is between 18 and 59 minutes. -/
theorem scenario_answer :
    ∀ mc cc mm cm hs ht : ℝ,
    ((24 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((100 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ mc → mc ≤ ((26 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((100 : ℕ) : ℝ) →
    ((4180 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ cc → cc ≤ ((4220 : ℕ) : ℝ) →
    ((18 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((100 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ mm → mm ≤ ((22 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((100 : ℕ) : ℝ) →
    ((750 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ cm → cm ≤ ((900 : ℕ) : ℝ) →
    ((8 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ hs → hs ≤ ((16 : ℕ) : ℝ) → ((20 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ ht → ht ≤ ((70 : ℕ) : ℝ) →
    ∃ tau : ℝ, ((18 : ℕ) : ℝ) < tau ∧ tau < ((59 : ℕ) : ℝ) ∧
      coffee (heatCapacity mc cc mm cm) (hs * sideArea + ht * topArea) ((0 : ℕ) : ℝ) = ((90 : ℕ) : ℝ) ∧
      (∀ t : ℝ, coffee (heatCapacity mc cc mm cm)
        (hs * sideArea + ht * topArea) t = ((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) ↔ t = tau) ∧
      (∀ t : ℝ, coffee (heatCapacity mc cc mm cm)
        (hs * sideArea + ht * topArea) t ≤ ((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) ↔ tau ≤ t) := by
  intro mc cc mm cm hs ht hmcl hmcu hccl hccu hmml hmmu hcml hcmu hslo hshi htlo hthi
  norm_num only [Nat.cast_ofNat] at hmcl hmcu hccl hccu hmml hmmu hcml hcmu hslo hshi htlo hthi ⊢
  obtain ⟨hClo, hChi⟩ := capacity_bounds mc cc mm cm (by linarith) (by linarith) hccl hccu (by linarith) (by linarith) hcml hcmu
  obtain ⟨hAslo, hAshi, hAtlo, hAthi⟩ := area_bounds
  let C := heatCapacity mc cc mm cm
  let G := hs * sideArea + ht * topArea
  have hAs : 0 ≤ sideArea := by linarith
  have hAt : 0 ≤ topArea := by linarith
  have hsl := mul_le_mul hslo hAslo (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 138 / 10000)
    (by linarith : 0 ≤ hs)
  have htl := mul_le_mul htlo hAtlo (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 502 / 100000)
    (by linarith : 0 ≤ ht)
  have hsu := mul_le_mul hshi hAshi hAs (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 16)
  have htu := mul_le_mul hthi hAthi hAt (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 70)
  have hGlo : (2108 / 10000 : ℝ) ≤ G := by dsimp [G]; linarith
  have hGhi : G ≤ (57338 / 100000 : ℝ) := by dsimp [G]; linarith
  have hC : 0 < C := by dsimp [C]; linarith
  have hG : 0 < G := by linarith
  have hden : 0 < 60 * G := by positivity
  obtain ⟨hLlo, hLhi⟩ := logarithm_bounds
  let tau := C * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G)
  have hlo : 18 < tau := by
    dsimp [tau]
    rw [lt_div_iff₀ hden]
    have hprod := mul_lt_mul_of_pos_left hLlo hC
    change 11382 / 10 ≤ C at hClo
    nlinarith
  have hhi : tau < 59 := by
    dsimp [tau]
    rw [div_lt_iff₀ hden]
    have hprod := mul_lt_mul_of_pos_left hLhi hC
    change C ≤ 12952 / 10 at hChi
    nlinarith
  exact ⟨tau, hlo, hhi, initial_temperature C G,
    (first_crossing C G hC hG).1, (first_crossing C G hC hG).2⟩

/- Nominal masses .25/.20 kg, specific heats 4200/800 J/(kg K),
side/top coefficients 14/35 W/(m^2 K), exact cylindrical areas above.
These are scenario values, not measurements. First crossing: 30..32 minutes. -/
theorem nominal_answer :
    ∃ tau : ℝ, ((30 : ℕ) : ℝ) < tau ∧ tau < ((32 : ℕ) : ℝ) ∧
      (∀ t : ℝ, coffee (heatCapacity (((1 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((4 : ℕ) : ℝ)) ((4200 : ℕ) : ℝ) (((1 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((5 : ℕ) : ℝ)) ((800 : ℕ) : ℝ))
        (((14 : ℕ) : ℝ) * sideArea + ((35 : ℕ) : ℝ) * topArea) t = ((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) ↔ t = tau) ∧
      (∀ t : ℝ, coffee (heatCapacity (((1 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((4 : ℕ) : ℝ)) ((4200 : ℕ) : ℝ) (((1 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((5 : ℕ) : ℝ)) ((800 : ℕ) : ℝ))
        (((14 : ℕ) : ℝ) * sideArea + ((35 : ℕ) : ℝ) * topArea) t ≤ ((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) ↔ tau ≤ t) := by
  norm_num only [Nat.cast_ofNat]
  obtain ⟨hAslo, hAshi, hAtlo, hAthi⟩ := area_bounds
  let G := 14 * sideArea + 35 * topArea
  have hGlo : (3689 / 10000 : ℝ) ≤ G := by dsimp [G]; linarith
  have hGhi : G ≤ (36967 / 100000 : ℝ) := by dsimp [G]; linarith
  have hG : 0 < G := by linarith
  have hden : 0 < 60 * G := by positivity
  have hcross := first_crossing 1210 G (by norm_num) hG
  obtain ⟨hlo, hhi⟩ := logarithm_bounds
  refine ⟨1210 * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G), ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · rw [lt_div_iff₀ hden]
    nlinarith
  · rw [div_lt_iff₀ hden]
    nlinarith
  · norm_num [heatCapacity] at hcross ⊢
    exact hcross.1
  · norm_num [heatCapacity] at hcross ⊢
    exact hcross.2

Conditional answer: about 31 minutes for the chosen illustrative mug. The formal nominal result is a first crossing of 60 C strictly between 30 and 32 minutes. With the wider physical assumptions below, the formal bound is 18 to 59 minutes. Neither is an unconditional prediction or a measured interval for the user's cup. The initial temperature alone still does not identify its cooling rate.

REVIEW: SUPPLIED INFORMATION
Coffee starts at 90 C; the target is 60 C. There are no timed temperature readings, geometry measurements, or cooling coefficients. Operator response 64bece41241640479ea01e37c9617a2e supplies general NIST and COMSOL references and explicitly supplies no cooling curve or fitted rate. It permits independently constructed physical scenario assumptions. No measurement, physical experiment, or external cooling-time answer has been invented.

REVIEW: ADDITIONAL ASSUMPTIONS AND THEIR REASONS
Chosen geometry and environment: open cylindrical ceramic mug, internal radius exactly 0.04 m in the idealized geometry, wall thickness 0.004 m, outside radius 0.044 m, and fill/exposed-side height 0.05 m. These are chosen dimensions, not measured facts. The modeled side is the outer cylinder alongside the liquid: As=2*pi*(0.04+0.004)*0.05=0.0044*pi m^2. The open liquid top is At=pi*0.04^2=0.0016*pi m^2. Thus the chosen liquid volume is At*0.05, approximately 251.3 mL. Areas are evaluated from geometry rather than assigned independently. The formal proof encloses As in [0.0138,0.01383] and At in [0.00502,0.00503] m^2; rounded bounds enclose the exact values and do not redefine them. Neglect base, rim, handle and wall above liquid; choose an insulating coaster, constant 20 C air and radiative surroundings, still air, no lid/heating, and sufficient mixing. Assume a mug preheated to 90 C and a common mug/liquid temperature. A cold mug would require a different initial model.

Chosen component storage ranges (all scenario assumptions, not measurements): coffee mass mc in [0.24,0.26] kg, coffee specific heat cc in [4180,4220] J/(kg K), mug mass mm in [0.18,0.22] kg, ceramic specific heat cm in [750,900] J/(kg K). Nominal values are mc=.25, cc=4200, mm=.20, cm=800. The coffee mass is consistent with roughly 251 mL of water-like liquid (implied density roughly 955-1035 kg/m^3); allowing an independent mass box is a conservative approximation rather than a density measurement or an assertion that every corner is physically realizable. The chosen mug mass is plausible for a small ceramic vessel including its base and handle. The material-specific mug heat capacity remains an assumption: no ceramic measurement has been supplied. Its range represents illustrative material uncertainty, not a literature-certified bound for all ceramics.

Treat coffee as water-like: the NIST correlation [1] gives approximately 4182, 4192, 4206 J/(kg K) at 60,75,90 C. The chosen constant effective coffee specific-heat range brackets this scale; constancy and application to coffee remain approximations. It is not fitted to cooling observations. Define heatCapacity(mc,cc,mm,cm)=mc*cc+mm*cm. capacity_bounds derives [1138.2,1295.2] J/K from the component intervals using positivity and multiplication. No independent assumption about total C is used. Nominal C=1210 J/K. Ignoring small mass loss and treating these effective capacities as constant are explicit model choices.

Chosen heat loss: hs is an effective side heat-transfer coefficient including convection, radiation and wall resistance. ht is an effective open-top coefficient including convection, radiation AND evaporation. Units are W/(m^2 K). Independent heat-loss paths add, giving total conductance G = hs*As + ht*At W/K. The small bottom and handle paths are neglected in this chosen insulated-base model.

For the nominal scenario take hs=14 and ht=35. These are engineering approximations chosen before calculating the crossing time, not inferred from the 90 C datum or adjusted to reproduce a time. Reasons and limitations:
- The generic vertical-wall natural-convection correlation documented by COMSOL [2], evaluated at a representative 75 C hot side and a height of 5 cm, with chosen approximate air properties beta=1/325 K^-1, nu=1.8e-5 m^2/s, alpha=2.6e-5 m^2/s, k_air=0.028 W/(m K), gives Ra=4.43e5 and h_conv about 7.8 W/(m^2 K). Ra is in its stated range. The correlation Nu=0.68+0.670 Ra^(1/4)/(1+(0.492/Pr)^(9/16))^(4/9) is used as a planar approximation to the cylindrical side, not an exact cup calculation. These air properties and the geometry approximation are extra choices.
- Gray-surface radiation [3], with chosen emissivity 0.9, gives h_rad=epsilon*sigma*(T+Ta)*(T^2+Ta^2) about 6.8 W/(m^2 K) at 75 C against 20 C. Adding convection and radiation and including a chosen 4 mm wall of conductivity 1.5 W/(m K) in series gives about 14.0 W/(m^2 K). The wall conductivity and emissivity are assumptions, not independently verified ceramic measurements.
- For the top, use approximately 7 convection + 7 radiation + 21 evaporation = 35 W/(m^2 K). The evaporative part matters. For a plausibility calculation, use the generic flux law j=km*(rho_sat-rho_air), latent flux Lv*j [4], choose km=0.0022 m/s, relative humidity 50% at 20 C, and Lv=2.3e6 J/kg. NIST Antoine vapor-pressure correlations [5] and ideal-gas vapor densities give a representative evaporative coefficient about 21 W/(m^2 K) at 75 C. This is a calculation under a chosen mass-transfer coefficient, not a measurement of evaporation. km corresponds roughly to a centimeter-scale diffusive boundary layer, a plausible but unverified still-air scenario. The calculated whole-surface loss is about 0.15 g/min, so ignoring the small total mass decrease is another approximation.
- Evaporation, radiation and convection vary with temperature, humidity, cup rim, air motion and surface conditions. Replacing them by constants over 90 to 60 C is a linearized engineering model, not a physical law proved by Lean. For sensitivity, explicitly ASSUME hs in [8,16] and ht in [20,70], CONSTANT over the cooling interval. This makes G in approximately [0.211115,0.573027] W/K. These broad ranges reflect mechanism-scale uncertainty, especially evaporation; literature does not certify them as bounds for the real coffee. The real cup may lie outside them. They are sufficient conditional physical inputs for a numerical example and are open to human review.

REVIEW: NECESSARY DEFINITIONS AND SCIENTIFIC MODEL
The necessary definitions are heatCapacity and the geometric sideArea/topArea just described, together with coffee(C,G,t) = 20 + (90-20)*exp(-(60*G/C)*t), in Celsius, with t in minutes, C in J/K, G in W/K. Physical use restricts t>=0. The formal algebraic family extends to all real t. The lumped energy balance is C*dT/dt = -60*G*(T-20). Constant heat capacity and constant linear total conductance give this exponential family. heat_balance proves it actually satisfies that differential balance, and initial_temperature proves the supplied initial condition. The choice of this family is conditional, not inferred from a cooling curve. No Bayesian prior, noise distribution or stochastic observation model is introduced: this is a deterministic initial-value prediction with exact 90 C, not a fit to absent observations. The ranges specify a set of scenarios, not a probability distribution.

REVIEW: PROVED CONCLUSIONS
first_crossing derives the unique first crossing t60 = C*ln(7/4)/(60*G) for every C>0 and G>0 by inverting the exponential. It also proves T(t)<=60 iff t>=t60, so the result is a first crossing and not merely equality at an arbitrary time. logarithm_bounds proves 0.55<ln(7/4)<0.57 from logarithm monotonicity, log(x)<=x-1, and exact rational powers; no logarithm value is a premise.

scenario_answer: for every component mass/specific-heat tuple in the stated box, hs in [8,16] and ht in [20,70], the model starts at 90 C and first crosses 60 C strictly between 18 and 59 minutes. Derived total C lies in [1138.2,1295.2]. The proof derives G bounds [0.2108,0.57338] W/K from proved area enclosures and coefficient bounds, then propagates C and G through the solved exponential model. Using exact pi and the box endpoints gives about 18.526 and 57.221 minutes; these decimal values are explanatory calculations, while 18..59 is the conservative formally certified enclosure.

nominal_answer: with component values .25 kg,4200 J/(kg K),.20 kg,800 J/(kg K), and hs=14, ht=35, the first 60 C crossing is strictly between 30 and 32 minutes. Geometry yields G=0.1176*pi W/K, approximately .369451 W/K. The exact solved time is 1210*ln(7/4)/(60*0.1176*pi), approximately 30.5469 minutes. The formal proof uses the derived geometric areas and proves both uniqueness and the threshold inequality. No cooling time or fitted rate is assumed. Decimal calculations and mechanism scale estimates are reproduced in scenario_calculation.py and cycle4-calculations.log; their detailed physical validity is not proved in Lean.

The two final theorems are distinct: one propagates parameter uncertainty across the chosen box, the other proves the nominal numerical worked example. No extra axioms or sorry are used. Modeling choices appear directly in coffee, the theorem parameters and numerical constants; technical normalization and threshold algebra are proved in helpers. Lean correctness establishes the conditional mathematical inference, not the truth of the physical assumptions or human acceptance. To calibrate the user's actual cup, measure ambient temperature and several timed liquid temperatures; those data would identify and test an effective cooling model. None are available here.
