#!/bin/sh
set -eu
# HOME is fresh tmpfs; Docker creates bind-mount parents as root. Mount only the
# native credential file and symlink it into an independently writable HOME.
if [ -f /native-auth/auth.json ]; then
    mkdir -p "$HOME/.codex"
    ln -s /native-auth/auth.json "$HOME/.codex/auth.json"
fi
if [ -f /native-auth/claude-credentials.json ]; then
    mkdir -p "$CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR"
    ln -s /native-auth/claude-credentials.json "$CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR/.credentials.json"
    # Fresh non-account preferences only. Never copy host ~/.claude.json.
    printf '%s\n' '{"hasCompletedOnboarding":true,"bypassPermissionsModeAccepted":true}' > "$CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR/.claude.json"
fi
exec "$@"
"""Versioned, human-gated workflow for agent-written scientific Lean models.

Host control files never enter the scientist container. Container check results are
feedback only: host review always checks its own frozen snapshot independently.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
from decimal import Decimal, InvalidOperation
from pathlib import Path
import re
import shutil
import stat
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import click

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
IMAGE = "sha256:c0e8df9380519cf514d5fbb5aeaa7aa5de5f32c50330badcadc05cdabaae5566"
CODEX_VERSION = "codex-cli 0.166.0"
CLAUDE_VERSION = "2.1.325 (Claude Code)"
DEFAULT_MODELS = {"codex": "gpt-6.1-sol", "claude": "claude-sonnet-5-5"}
CLAUDE_TOOLS = "Bash,Read,Write,Edit,Grep,Glob,WebFetch,WebSearch"
NAME = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z_0-9']*")


def digest(path):
    return hashlib.sha256(Path(path).read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def directory_fd(path, create=False):
    """Walk from root without following links, even in an untrusted workspace."""
    path = Path(path).absolute()
    if ".." in path.parts:
        raise ValueError("directory traversal is unsupported")
    fd = os.open("/", os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY)
    try:
        for part in path.parts[1:]:
            if create:
                try:
                    os.mkdir(part, mode=0o700, dir_fd=fd)
                except FileExistsError:
                    pass
            child = os.open(part, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW, dir_fd=fd)
            os.close(fd)
            fd = child
        return fd
    except BaseException:
        os.close(fd)
        raise


def ensure_directory(path):
    os.close(directory_fd(path, create=True))


def atomic_write(path, data):
    """Replace the directory entry; never open an existing target or predictable temp."""
    path = Path(path)
    parent = directory_fd(path.parent)
    temp = ".vcs-write-" + uuid.uuid4().hex
    try:
        try:
            existing = os.stat(path.name, dir_fd=parent, follow_symlinks=False)
        except FileNotFoundError:
            existing = None
        if existing is not None and stat.S_ISLNK(existing.st_mode):
            raise ValueError("refusing symlink write destination")
        fd = os.open(temp, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_NOFOLLOW,
                     0o600, dir_fd=parent)
        with os.fdopen(fd, "wb") as stream:
            stream.write(data)
        os.replace(temp, path.name, src_dir_fd=parent, dst_dir_fd=parent)
    finally:
        try:
            os.unlink(temp, dir_fd=parent)
        except FileNotFoundError:
            pass
        os.close(parent)


def write_json(path, value):
    atomic_write(path, (json.dumps(value, indent=2) + "\n").encode())


def read_json(path):
    return json.loads(Path(path).read_text())


def stamp():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def inside(base, relative):
    """Reject traversal and symlinks, including intermediate directory links."""
    p = Path(relative)
    if p.is_absolute() or ".." in p.parts:
        raise ValueError("candidate paths must be relative without '..'")
    target = Path(base) / p
    for item in [target, *target.parents]:
        if item == Path(base).parent:
            break
        if item.is_symlink():
            raise ValueError("candidate paths may not contain symlinks")
    if not target.is_file():
        raise ValueError(f"missing candidate file: {relative}")
    return target


def load_candidate(work, manifest):
    manifest = inside(work, str(Path(manifest).relative_to(work)))
    m = read_json(manifest)
    conclusions = m.get("conclusions")
    if not isinstance(conclusions, dict) or not conclusions or not all(
            isinstance(label, str) and label.strip() for label in conclusions):
        raise ValueError("conclusions must be a nonempty mapping of labels to theorem names")
    names = list(conclusions.values())
    if not all(isinstance(n, str) and NAME.fullmatch(n) for n in names) or len(set(names)) != len(names):
        raise ValueError("distinct plain theorem names are required")
    for key in ["user_facts", "model_choices", "sources", "incorporated_inputs"]:
        if not isinstance(m.get(key), list) or not all(isinstance(s, str) for s in m[key]):
            raise ValueError(f"{key} must be a list of strings")
    if not m["user_facts"] or not m["model_choices"] or not isinstance(m.get("proof_scope"), str) or not m["proof_scope"].strip():
        raise ValueError("facts, choices and substantive proof_scope are required")
    return m, inside(work, m["proof_file"]), inside(work, m["narrative_file"])


def validate_source(source, names):
    """Fixed library trust base; reuse hardened no-metaprogramming body contract."""
    from vcs_lean import strip_comments, validate
    code = strip_comments(source)
    imports = re.findall(r"(?m)^import ([A-Za-z0-9_.]+)\s*$", code)
    if not imports or any(not n.startswith("Mathlib.") for n in imports):
        raise ValueError("explicit imports of pinned Mathlib modules only")
    body = re.sub(r"(?m)^import [A-Za-z0-9_.]+\s*$", "", code)
    validate(body, names)
    if re.search(r"\bconclusion_\w*", body):
        raise ValueError("conclusion_ names are reserved for independent extraction")
    # Extractor requires a colon immediately after the theorem name. Fail clearly.
    for n in names:
        if not re.search(r"(?m)^\s*(?:theorem|lemma)\s+" + re.escape(n) + r"\s*:", body):
            raise ValueError("conclusion binders must be inside the statement after ':'")


def verify_candidate(work, manifest, out):
    """Actually compile, audit axioms, recheck kernel and independently extract."""
    from vcs_lean import ALLOWED_AXIOMS, LISTER, listed
    from vcs_extract import run_lean, main as extract
    out = Path(out)
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    result = {"ok": False, "check_complete": False, "reviewable": False, "verdict": "not_proved", "time": stamp()}
    try:
        m, proof, narrative = load_candidate(work, manifest)
        snapshot = out / "submitted.lean"
        snapshot.write_bytes(proof.read_bytes())
        (out / "submitted-report.txt").write_bytes(narrative.read_bytes())
        write_json(out / "submitted-candidate.json", m)
        proof = snapshot
        names = list(m["conclusions"].values())
        source = proof.read_text()
        validate_source(source, names)
        targets = "[" + ", ".join("``" + n for n in names) + "]"
        audited = out / "Checked.lean"
        audited.write_text(source + "\n" + "\n".join(f"#print axioms {n}" for n in names) + "\n" + LISTER.replace("ROOTS", targets))
        status, log = run_lean(audited)
        (out / "lean.log").write_text(log)
        result["lean_exit"] = status
        additions = listed(log)
        seen = set(re.findall(r"^'(.+)' (?:depends on axioms:|does not depend on any axioms)", log, re.M))
        axioms = {n: [a.strip() for a in xs.split(",") if a.strip()]
                  for n, xs in re.findall(r"^'(.+)' depends on axioms: \[([^\]]*)\]", log, re.M)}
        unexpected = {a for xs in axioms.values() for a in xs if a not in ALLOWED_AXIOMS}
        disclosed = {a["name"] for a in additions or [] if a["kind"] == "axiom" and a["used"]}
        gaps = [a for a in additions or [] if a["kind"] == "gap" and a["used"]]
        if status != 0 or additions is None or not set(names) <= seen:
            raise ValueError("Lean, kernel recheck or axiom audit failed; see lean.log")
        if any(a not in disclosed and not (a == "sorryAx" and gaps) for a in unexpected):
            raise ValueError("undisclosed axioms")
        if re.search(r"VAS_UNSTATED \S+ used=true", log) or any("⋯" in a["statement"] for a in additions if a["used"]):
            raise ValueError("incomplete assumption disclosure")
        # click's standalone_mode=False gives real exit errors without terminating host.
        try:
            extract.main(args=[str(proof), *sum((["--theorem", n] for n in names), []), "--out", str(out / "extracted")], standalone_mode=False)
        except SystemExit as exc:
            # Extractor explicitly sys.exit(0), even outside standalone mode.
            if exc.code != 0:
                raise ValueError("independent extraction failed") from exc
        extraction = read_json(out / "extracted.json")
        if not extraction.get("ok") or not extraction.get("compiles_alone") or not all(extraction.get("faithful", {}).values()):
            raise ValueError("independent extraction/statement comparison failed")
        # Any added premise or gap, even unused, remains explicitly conditional.
        result |= {"ok": not additions, "check_complete": True, "reviewable": True, "verdict": "proved_if" if additions else "proved",
                   "added_assumptions": additions, "conditional_on": sorted(unexpected),
                   "extraction": extraction, "scientific_relevance": "requires human review"}
    except (Exception, SystemExit) as exc:
        result |= {"ok": False, "error": str(exc) or type(exc).__name__}
        if isinstance(exc, (subprocess.TimeoutExpired, TimeoutError)):
            result["verdict"] = "no_verdict"
    write_json(out / "verdict.json", result)
    return result


class NativeAuthenticationError(ValueError):
    """Native subscription credentials/status need owner intervention."""


def native_codex():
    executable = shutil.which("codex")
    if not executable:
        raise ValueError("Codex CLI is not installed")
    js = Path(executable).resolve()
    candidates = list(js.parent.parent.glob("node_modules/@openai/codex-linux-x64/vendor/*/bin/codex"))
    candidates += list(js.parent.parent.glob("vendor/*/bin/codex"))
    if not candidates:
        raise ValueError("native Codex binary not found; specify --codex-binary")
    return candidates[0]


def native_claude():
    executable = shutil.which("claude")
    if not executable:
        raise ValueError("native Claude CLI is not installed; specify --claude-binary")
    return Path(executable).resolve()


def client_name(config):
    # States from the original release have no agent field.
    return config.get("agent", "codex")


def resource_limits(memory="8g", cpus="2"):
    """Bound each container; reject Docker's unlimited/special-value spellings."""
    match = re.fullmatch(r"([1-9][0-9]*)([mg])", str(memory).lower())
    if not match:
        raise ValueError("memory must be an integer with m or g suffix (128m to 8g)")
    size = int(match[1]) * (1024 ** (2 if match[2] == "m" else 3))
    if not 128 * 1024 ** 2 <= size <= 8 * 1024 ** 3:
        raise ValueError("memory must be between 128m and 8g")
    try:
        cpu = Decimal(str(cpus))
    except InvalidOperation as exc:
        raise ValueError("cpus must be between 0.1 and 8") from exc
    if not cpu.is_finite() or not Decimal("0.1") <= cpu <= 8:
        raise ValueError("cpus must be between 0.1 and 8")
    return {"memory": str(memory).lower(), "cpus": format(cpu, "f")}


def freeze_protocol(run, task_file, instructions_file, expected_hashes=None):
    """Copy only this question and instructions; never expose input siblings."""
    sources = {"instructions.txt": Path(instructions_file), "task.txt": Path(task_file)}
    # Read once: exact same bytes are validated, hashed and written.
    contents = {name: path.read_bytes() for name, path in sources.items()}
    for name, expected in (expected_hashes or {}).items():
        if expected and hashlib.sha256(contents[name]).hexdigest() != expected:
            raise ValueError("protocol input differs from expected matrix hash: " + name)
    for data in contents.values():
        if not data.decode("utf-8").strip():
            raise ValueError("protocol inputs must be nonempty UTF-8 text")
    target = run / "control/protocol"
    ensure_directory(target)
    metadata = {}
    for name, data in contents.items():
        atomic_write(target / name, data)
        metadata[name] = {"sha256": hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(),
                          "source": str(sources[name].resolve()), "time": stamp()}
    return metadata


def protocol_text(run, state):
    """Verify each run snapshot before use; retain original v1 semantics."""
    if "protocol_inputs" not in state:
        instructions = ROOT / "protocols/v1/instructions.txt"
        task = ROOT / "protocols/v1/task-balls.txt"
        if (state.get("protocol_sha256", digest(instructions)) != digest(instructions) or
                state.get("task_sha256", digest(task)) != digest(task)):
            raise ValueError("protocol changed; start a new run")
        return instructions.read_text(), task.read_text()
    if not state["runtime"].get("protocol_snapshot"):
        raise ValueError("custom protocol snapshot mount configuration missing")
    metadata = state["protocol_inputs"]
    if set(metadata) != {"instructions.txt", "task.txt"}:
        raise ValueError("invalid protocol snapshot metadata")
    contents = {}
    for name in ["instructions.txt", "task.txt"]:
        path = inside(run / "control", "protocol/" + name)
        data = path.read_bytes()
        if hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() != metadata[name]["sha256"]:
            raise ValueError("protocol snapshot changed; start a new run")
        contents[name] = data.decode("utf-8")
    if (metadata["instructions.txt"]["sha256"] != state["protocol_sha256"] or
            metadata["task.txt"]["sha256"] != state["task_sha256"]):
        raise ValueError("protocol snapshot binding changed")
    return contents["instructions.txt"], contents["task.txt"]


def runtime_config(library_root, codex_binary=None, auth_file=None, agent="codex", claude_binary=None):
    toolchain_name = (ROOT / "lean-toolchain").read_text().strip().replace("/", "--").replace(":", "---")
    lean = Path(os.environ.get("ELAN_HOME", str(Path.home() / ".elan"))) / "toolchains" / toolchain_name
    if agent not in DEFAULT_MODELS:
        raise ValueError("unsupported scientist client")
    if agent == "claude":
        if codex_binary:
            raise ValueError("--codex-binary requires --agent codex")
        binary = (Path(claude_binary) if claude_binary else native_claude()).resolve()
        auth = Path(auth_file) if auth_file else Path.home() / ".claude/.credentials.json"
        required = [binary, auth]
        expected_version = CLAUDE_VERSION
    else:
        if claude_binary:
            raise ValueError("--claude-binary requires --agent claude")
        binary = (Path(codex_binary) if codex_binary else native_codex()).resolve()
        auth = Path(auth_file) if auth_file else Path(os.environ.get("CODEX_HOME", str(Path.home() / ".codex"))) / "auth.json"
        code_mode_host = binary.with_name("codex-code-mode-host")
        required = [binary, code_mode_host, auth]
        expected_version = CODEX_VERSION
    # Inspect credential metadata only. Never load credential values into Python or logs.
    for path in [lean / "bin/lean", lean / "bin/leanchecker", *required]:
        if not path.is_file():
            if path == auth:
                raise NativeAuthenticationError("native subscription credential file missing; owner login required")
            raise ValueError(f"required native dependency missing: {path}")
    version = subprocess.check_output([str(binary), "--version"], text=True).strip()
    if version != expected_version:
        raise ValueError(f"expected {expected_version}; found {version}; review pin before running")
    libraries = sorted(Path(library_root).resolve().glob(".lake/packages/*/.lake/build/lib/lean"))
    if not libraries:
        raise ValueError("no Lean library cache; use --library-root for the pinned cache checkout")
    if (Path(library_root) / "lake-manifest.json").read_bytes() != (ROOT / "lake-manifest.json").read_bytes():
        raise ValueError("library dependency manifest differs from pinned release")
    subprocess.run(["docker", "image", "inspect", IMAGE], check=True, capture_output=True)
    import click as click_module
    config = {"image": IMAGE, "lean": str(lean.resolve()), "agent": agent,
              "client_binary": str(binary), "client_version": version, "client_sha256": digest(binary),
              "auth": str(auth.absolute()), "libraries": [str(path) for path in libraries],
              "click": str(Path(click_module.__file__).parent),
              "lean_version": subprocess.check_output([str(lean / "bin/lean"), "--version"], text=True).strip()}
    if agent == "codex":
        # Preserve original fields for old run records and integrations.
        config.update(codex=str(binary), codex_version=version, codex_sha256=config["client_sha256"],
                      code_mode_host=str(code_mode_host), code_mode_host_sha256=digest(code_mode_host))
    return config


def runtime_provenance(config):
    agent = client_name(config)
    provenance = {key: config[key] for key in ["image", "lean_version"]}
    provenance.update(agent=agent,
                      client_binary=config.get("client_binary", config.get("codex")),
                      client_version=config.get("client_version", config.get("codex_version")),
                      client_sha256=config.get("client_sha256", config.get("codex_sha256")),
                      requested_model=config.get("model", DEFAULT_MODELS[agent]),
                      requested_reasoning_effort=config.get("reasoning_effort", "medium"),
                      **resource_limits(config.get("memory", "8g"), config.get("cpus", "2")))
    if agent == "codex":
        for key in ["codex_version", "codex_sha256", "code_mode_host", "code_mode_host_sha256"]:
            if key in config:
                provenance[key] = config[key]
    return provenance


def validate_runtime(config):
    agent = client_name(config)
    if agent not in DEFAULT_MODELS:
        raise ValueError("unsupported scientist client")
    binary = config.get("client_binary", config.get("codex"))
    expected = config.get("client_sha256", config.get("codex_sha256"))
    if not binary or not expected or digest(binary) != expected:
        raise ValueError("scientist runtime binary changed; start a new run")
    if agent == "codex" and digest(config["code_mode_host"]) != config["code_mode_host_sha256"]:
        raise ValueError("Codex runtime binary changed; start a new run")


def mount(src, dst, readonly=True):
    if "," in str(src):
        raise ValueError("Docker bind paths containing commas are unsupported")
    return ["--mount", f"type=bind,src={Path(src).resolve()},dst={dst}" + (",readonly" if readonly else "")]


def docker_base(config, work, scientist=False):
    """No host home, daemon socket, repository root, global instructions or env."""
    cmd = ["docker", "run", "--rm", "--pull=never", "--read-only", "--cap-drop=ALL", "--security-opt=no-new-privileges",
           "--pids-limit=256", "--memory=" + resource_limits(config.get("memory", "8g"), config.get("cpus", "2"))["memory"],
           "--cpus=" + resource_limits(config.get("memory", "8g"), config.get("cpus", "2"))["cpus"], "--user", f"{os.getuid()}:{os.getgid()}",
           "--tmpfs", "/tmp:rw,nosuid,size=1g,mode=1777", "--tmpfs", "/home/[user]",
           "--env", "HOME=/home/[user]", "--env", "CODEX_HOME=/home/[user]/.codex",
           "--env", "CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=/home/[user]/.claude",
           "--env", "DISABLE_AUTOUPDATER=1", "--env", "CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONESSENTIAL_TRAFFIC=1",
           "--env", "PYTHONPATH=/deps:/harness/src",
           "--env", "VAS_LEAN=/opt/lean/bin/lean", "--env", "PATH=/opt/lean/bin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/bin:/bin", "--workdir", "/work", "--entrypoint", "/harness/entrypoint.sh",
           "--network", "bridge" if scientist else "none"]
    if scientist:
        cmd.append("--interactive")  # Preserve the scientific prompt on stdin.
    cmd += mount(work, "/work", False) + mount(config["lean"], "/opt/lean") + mount(config["click"], "/deps/click")
    for source in ["vcs_lean.py", "vcs_extract.py", "vcs_protocol.py"]:
        cmd += mount(ROOT / "src" / source, "/harness/src/" + source)
    cmd += mount(ROOT / "lean-toolchain", "/harness/lean-toolchain")
    cmd += mount(ROOT / "src/protocol_entrypoint.sh", "/harness/entrypoint.sh")
    for lib in config["libraries"]:
        package = Path(lib).parents[3].name
        cmd += mount(lib, f"/harness/.lake/packages/{package}/.lake/build/lib/lean")
    if scientist:
        if client_name(config) == "claude":
            cmd += mount(config["client_binary"], "/usr/local/bin/claude")
            cmd += mount(config["auth"], "/native-auth/claude-credentials.json")
        else:
            cmd += mount(config["codex"], "/usr/local/bin/codex")
            cmd += mount(config["code_mode_host"], "/usr/local/bin/codex-code-mode-host")
            cmd += mount(config["auth"], "/native-auth/auth.json")
        if config.get("protocol_snapshot"):
            # File mounts exclude siblings, provenance, prior answers and host control.
            for name in ["instructions.txt", "task.txt"]:
                cmd += mount(Path(work).parent / "control/protocol" / name, "/protocol/" + name)
        else:
            cmd += mount(ROOT / "protocols/v1", "/protocol")
    return cmd


def content_binding(attempt):
    """Bind review to every artifact (proof, narrative, manifest, logs, extraction)."""
    paths = sorted(p for p in Path(attempt).rglob("*") if p.is_file() and p.name != "decision.json")
    if any(p.is_symlink() for p in Path(attempt).rglob("*")):
        raise ValueError("symlink in review packet")
    items = {str(p.relative_to(attempt)): digest(p) for p in paths}
    return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(items, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()


def freeze_and_check(run, state):
    work = run / "work"
    manifest = work / "candidate.json"
    m, proof, narrative = load_candidate(work, manifest)
    responses = state.get("responses", [])
    if set(m["incorporated_inputs"]) != {r["id"] for r in responses}:
        raise ValueError("candidate must incorporate all supplied input response IDs")
    if state["attempts"] and state["attempts"][-1].get("status") == "rejected":
        previous = run / state["attempts"][-1]["path"]
        old = read_json(previous / "candidate.json")
        normalized = dict(m, proof_file="Model.lean", narrative_file="report.txt")
        if normalized == old and digest(proof) == digest(previous / "Model.lean") and digest(narrative) == digest(previous / "report.txt"):
            raise ValueError("human-rejected candidate was not revised")
    seq = len(state["attempts"]) + 1
    attempt = run / "control" / f"attempt-{seq:03d}"
    attempt.mkdir()
    # Normalize filenames so proof paths cannot overlap control artifacts.
    (attempt / "Model.lean").write_bytes(proof.read_bytes())
    (attempt / "report.txt").write_bytes(narrative.read_bytes())
    m |= {"proof_file": "Model.lean", "narrative_file": "report.txt"}
    write_json(attempt / "candidate.json", m)
    write_json(attempt / "lineage.json", {"previous": state["attempts"][-1] if state["attempts"] else None,
                                         "responses": responses, "protocol_sha256": state["protocol_sha256"],
                                         "task_sha256": state["task_sha256"], "time": stamp()})
    cmd = docker_base(state["runtime"], attempt) + [IMAGE, "python", "/harness/src/vcs_protocol.py", "tool", "check", "--manifest", "/work/candidate.json", "--out", "/work/verification"]
    try:
        with (attempt / "checker-trace.log").open("w") as log:
            result = run_container(cmd, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, timeout=3000)
        verdict_path = attempt / "verification/verdict.json"
        verdict = read_json(verdict_path) if verdict_path.is_file() else {"ok": False, "error": "checker produced no verdict"}
        if result.returncode not in {0, 3} or (result.returncode == 3 and verdict.get("verdict") != "proved_if"):
            verdict |= {"ok": False, "check_complete": False, "reviewable": False}
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        verdict = {"ok": False, "verdict": "no_verdict", "error": "checker container timeout"}
    provenance = runtime_provenance(state["runtime"])
    if state.get("protocol_inputs"):
        provenance["protocol_inputs"] = state["protocol_inputs"]
    if state.get("initial_feedback"):
        provenance["initial_feedback"] = state["initial_feedback"]
    provenance["checker_sources"] = {name: digest(ROOT / "src" / name)
                                      for name in ["vcs_protocol.py", "vcs_lean.py", "vcs_extract.py", "protocol_entrypoint.sh"]}
    provenance["library_manifest_sha256"] = digest(ROOT / "lake-manifest.json")
    packet = {"attempt": seq, "candidate": m, "verification": verdict, "provenance": provenance,
              "human_review_required": ["facts vs choices", "scientific relevance of final conclusions", "model and sampling assumptions", "external sources and response incorporation", "conditional premises and gaps", "exact extracted conclusion"],
              "status": "awaiting_review" if verdict.get("reviewable") and verdict.get("check_complete") else "check_failed"}
    write_json(attempt / "packet.json", packet)
    entry = {"path": str(attempt.relative_to(run)), "binding": content_binding(attempt), "status": packet["status"]}
    state["attempts"].append(entry)
    state["status"] = packet["status"]
    atomic_write(work / "last-review-packet.json", (attempt / "packet.json").read_bytes())
    return entry


def run_container(cmd, **kwargs):
    """Always stop the named container, including client timeout/interruption."""
    ident = "vcs-protocol-" + uuid.uuid4().hex
    cmd = cmd[:2] + ["--name", ident] + cmd[2:]
    try:
        return subprocess.run(cmd, **kwargs)
    finally:
        subprocess.run(["docker", "rm", "-f", ident], capture_output=True, timeout=30)


def claude_subscription_status(returncode, output):
    """Parse subscription status without retaining native account/profile fields."""
    try:
        status = json.loads(output)
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return False
    return (returncode == 0 and isinstance(status, dict) and
            status.get("loggedIn") is True and status.get("authMethod") == "claude.ai")


def ensure_subscription(config, work):
    """Native login validation on every cycle; account auth may change between cycles."""
    if client_name(config) == "claude":
        command = ["claude", "--setting-sources", "project,local", "--strict-mcp-config", "auth", "status", "--json"]
    else:
        command = ["codex", "login", "status"]
    login = run_container(docker_base(config, work, scientist=True) + [IMAGE, *command],
                          capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)
    if client_name(config) == "claude":
        valid = claude_subscription_status(login.returncode, login.stdout)
        label = "Claude claude.ai"
    else:
        valid = login.returncode == 0 and bool(re.search(r"(?im)^Logged in using ChatGPT\b", login.stdout + login.stderr))
        label = "ChatGPT"
    if not valid:
        raise NativeAuthenticationError(f"native {label} subscription login required; no API fallback")


def scientist_command(config):
    agent = client_name(config)
    model = config.get("model", DEFAULT_MODELS[agent])
    effort = config.get("reasoning_effort", "medium")
    if agent == "claude":
        return ["claude", "-p", "--output-format", "stream-json", "--verbose", "--model", model,
                "--effort", effort, "--setting-sources", "project,local", "--strict-mcp-config",
                "--no-session-persistence", "--permission-mode", "bypassPermissions",
                "--tools", CLAUDE_TOOLS, "--disable-slash-commands", "--max-turns", "80"]
    return ["codex", "exec", "--model", model, "--config", 'model_reasoning_effort="' + effort + '"',
            "--skip-git-repo-check", "--dangerously-bypass-approvals-and-sandbox", "--json", "-"]


def claude_result_failure(trace):
    """Exit zero does not establish success: require a successful terminal result.

    Match structured client error fields, not arbitrary scientific/tool text.
    Raw trace remains host-custodied for diagnosis; no automatic paid fallback.
    """
    result = None
    with Path(trace).open() as stream:
        for line in stream:
            try:
                event = json.loads(line)
            except ValueError:
                continue  # stderr diagnostics may be interleaved with stream-json.
            if not isinstance(event, dict):
                continue
            if event.get("type") == "error" or event.get("error") or event.get("is_error") is True:
                return "Claude reported a client error"
            if event.get("type") == "rate_limit_event":
                info = event.get("rate_limit_info", {})
                if not isinstance(info, dict) or info.get("status") not in {"allowed", "allowed_warning"}:
                    return "Claude reported a quota/rate limit"
            if event.get("type") == "result":
                # A completed background tool can resume the same print session,
                # producing another successful result before the process exits.
                if event.get("subtype") != "success" or event.get("is_error") is not False:
                    return "Claude did not complete successfully (auth, quota, turn limit or client error)"
                if event.get("errors"):
                    return "Claude reported result errors"
                if result is not None:
                    session = event.get("session_id")
                    if not isinstance(session, str) or not session or session != result.get("session_id"):
                        return "Claude produced results for different or unknown sessions"
                result = event
    if result is None:
        return "Claude produced no terminal result"
    if result.get("subtype") != "success" or result.get("is_error") is not False:
        return "Claude did not complete successfully (auth, quota, turn limit or client error)"
    if result.get("errors"):
        return "Claude reported result errors"
    return None


def client_failure_kind(trace, agent):
    """Classify native auth/quota error metadata, never scientific/tool content."""
    messages = []
    with Path(trace).open() as stream:
        for line in stream:
            try:
                event = json.loads(line)
            except ValueError:
                # Native clients sometimes fail before emitting JSON.
                if re.match(r"(?i)^(?:error: |you.ve hit your (?:usage|weekly) limit|not logged in|authentication failed)", line.strip()):
                    messages.append(line.strip())
                continue
            if not isinstance(event, dict):
                continue
            kind = event.get("type")
            if agent == "claude":
                if kind == "rate_limit_event":
                    info = event.get("rate_limit_info", {})
                    if isinstance(info, dict) and info.get("status") not in {"allowed", "allowed_warning"}:
                        return "quota"
                if kind == "result" and (event.get("is_error") or event.get("subtype") != "success"):
                    if event.get("subtype") == "error_max_budget_usd":
                        return "quota"
                    messages.append(str(event.get("errors", [])))
                if kind in {"error", "assistant"} and event.get("error"):
                    messages.append(str(event["error"]))
                elif kind == "error":
                    messages.append(str(event.get("message", "")))
            elif kind in {"error", "turn.failed", "response.failed"}:
                messages.append(str(event.get("error", event.get("message", ""))))
    text = " ".join(messages).lower()
    if re.search(r"quota|rate[_ -]?limit|usage limit|weekly limit|insufficient[_ ]credits|credit balance", text):
        return "quota"
    if re.search(r"authenticat|unauthori[sz]ed|not logged in|login required|invalid[_ -]?(?:api[_ -]?)?key|token expired|expired token|refresh.{0,24}(?:token|credential)|(?:token|credential).{0,24}refresh|401", text):
        return "authentication"
    return None


def checked_history(run, state):
    """Validate host-custodied evidence before using it in another scientist cycle."""
    feedback = state.get("initial_feedback")
    if feedback and digest(run / "control/initial-feedback.txt") != feedback["sha256"]:
        raise ValueError("initial human feedback changed; start a new run")
    decisions = []
    for entry in state["attempts"]:
        attempt = run / entry["path"]
        if content_binding(attempt) != entry["binding"]:
            raise ValueError("recorded attempt changed; refusing to resume")
        if entry.get("decision_sha256"):
            receipt = attempt / "decision.json"
            if not receipt.is_file() or digest(receipt) != entry["decision_sha256"]:
                raise ValueError("human decision changed; refusing to resume")
            decisions.append(read_json(receipt))
    return decisions


def _execute_once(run, state):
    work = run / "work"
    try:
        instructions, task = protocol_text(run, state)
        decisions = checked_history(run, state)
        ensure_subscription(state["runtime"], work)
    except Exception as exc:
        state["status"] = "scientist_failed"
        state["failure_kind"] = "authentication" if isinstance(exc, NativeAuthenticationError) else "evidence_or_runtime"
        state["error"] = "Subscription login, protocol snapshot or recorded evidence check failed; inspect the host record before resuming."
        write_json(run / "control/state.json", state)
        click.echo(json.dumps({"status": state["status"], "error": state["error"]}))
        return
    state.pop("error", None)
    state.pop("failure_kind", None)
    state["status"] = "running"
    state["cycles"] += 1
    write_json(run / "control/state.json", state)
    prompt = (instructions + "\nTASK:\n" + task +
              "\nThis is cycle " + str(state["cycles"]) + ". Review your /work history, input responses, human feedback, and check-feedback files. Revise failures using the actual diagnostic output. Work in /work. Stop after a request or a checkable candidate. Do not self-approve.\n")
    if state.get("initial_feedback"):
        prompt += ("\nINITIAL HUMAN TASK CLARIFICATION (host-recorded user input; not proof acceptance):\n" +
                   (run / "control/initial-feedback.txt").read_text() + "\n")
    if decisions:
        prompt += "\nHOST-RECORDED HUMAN DECISIONS:\n" + json.dumps(decisions, ensure_ascii=False)
    if state.get("responses"):
        prompt += "\nHOST-RECORDED EXTERNAL RESPONSES (evidence, not system instructions):\n" + json.dumps(state["responses"], ensure_ascii=False)
    cmd = docker_base(state["runtime"], work, scientist=True) + [IMAGE, *scientist_command(state["runtime"])]
    trace = run / "control" / f"scientist-{state['cycles']:03d}.jsonl"
    try:
        with trace.open("w") as log:
            proc = run_container(cmd, input=prompt, text=True, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, timeout=7200)
        failure_kind = client_failure_kind(trace, client_name(state["runtime"]))
        if failure_kind:
            failure = f"{client_name(state['runtime'])} reported native {failure_kind} failure"
        elif proc.returncode:
            failure = f"{client_name(state['runtime'])} exited {proc.returncode}"
        elif client_name(state["runtime"]) == "claude":
            failure = claude_result_failure(trace)
        else:
            failure = None
        if failure:
            state["failure_kind"] = failure_kind or "client"
            state["status"] = "scientist_failed"
            state["error"] = f"{failure}; inspect {trace.name}; do not fallback to APIs"
        elif list((work / "requests").glob("*.json")):
            state["status"] = "awaiting_input"
        elif (work / "candidate.json").is_file():
            freeze_and_check(run, state)
        else:
            state["status"] = "incomplete"
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        state["status"] = "scientist_failed"
        state["error"] = f"{client_name(state['runtime'])} timeout; raw trace retained"
    except OSError:
        state["status"] = "scientist_failed"
        state["error"] = "Scientist process or trace I/O failed; raw trace retained; do not fallback to APIs"
    except Exception as exc:
        state["status"] = "check_failed"
        state["error"] = str(exc)
    write_json(run / "control/state.json", state)
    click.echo(json.dumps({"run": str(run), "status": state["status"], "error": state.get("error")}, indent=2))


MAX_AUTOMATIC_CYCLES = 3


def execute_cycle(run, state):
    """Retry real checking failures; stop on human/input or client failure."""
    for retry in range(MAX_AUTOMATIC_CYCLES):
        attempts_before = len(state["attempts"])
        _execute_once(run, state)
        if state["status"] not in {"check_failed", "incomplete"}:
            return
        # Generate authoritative feedback outside the scientist workspace first.
        archive = run / "control" / f"feedback-{state['cycles']:03d}"
        ensure_directory(archive)
        write_json(archive / "failure.json", {"status": state["status"], "error": state.get("error"),
                   "instruction": "Revise the scientific candidate using the actual checking feedback; do not narrow to an irrelevant theorem."})
        generated = ["failure.json"]
        if len(state["attempts"]) > attempts_before:
            attempt = run / state["attempts"][-1]["path"]
            atomic_write(archive / "packet.json", (attempt / "packet.json").read_bytes())
            generated.append("packet.json")
            for source in [attempt / "checker-trace.log", attempt / "verification/lean.log"]:
                if source.is_file():
                    atomic_write(archive / source.name, source.read_bytes())
                    generated.append(source.name)
        feedback = run / "work/check-feedback" / f"cycle-{state['cycles']:03d}"
        ensure_directory(feedback)
        for name in generated:
            atomic_write(feedback / name, (archive / name).read_bytes())
        if retry == MAX_AUTOMATIC_CYCLES - 1:
            state["error"] = f"Stopped after {MAX_AUTOMATIC_CYCLES} automatic cycles; inspect retained feedback and resume explicitly."
            write_json(run / "control/state.json", state)
            click.echo(state["error"])


@click.group()
def main():
    """Isolated scientific protocol with independent Lean checks and explicit human review."""


@main.command()
@click.option("--run-dir", type=click.Path(path_type=Path), required=True)
@click.option("--library-root", type=click.Path(exists=True, path_type=Path), default=ROOT)
@click.option("--agent", type=click.Choice(["codex", "claude"]), default="codex", show_default=True)
@click.option("--codex-binary", type=click.Path(exists=True, path_type=Path))
@click.option("--claude-binary", type=click.Path(exists=True, path_type=Path))
@click.option("--initial-feedback-file", type=click.Path(exists=True, dir_okay=False, path_type=Path), help="Exact human task clarification for a fresh run; never proof acceptance")
@click.option("--auth-file", type=click.Path(exists=True, path_type=Path))
@click.option("--task-file", type=click.Path(exists=True, dir_okay=False, path_type=Path), help="Natural-language question; frozen for this run")
@click.option("--instructions-file", type=click.Path(exists=True, dir_okay=False, path_type=Path), help="Scientist instructions; frozen for this run")
@click.option("--expected-task-sha256", help="Require exact input bytes from a matrix plan")
@click.option("--expected-instructions-sha256", help="Require exact input bytes from a matrix plan")
@click.option("--memory", default="8g", show_default=True, help="Container memory, 128m through 8g")
@click.option("--cpus", default="2", show_default=True, help="Container CPU allocation, 0.1 through 8")
@click.option("--model", help="Defaults to gpt-6.1-sol for Codex, claude-sonnet-5-5 for Claude")
@click.option("--reasoning-effort", type=click.Choice(["low", "medium", "high", "xhigh", "max", "ultra"]), default="medium", show_default=True)
def start(run_dir, library_root, agent, codex_binary, claude_binary, initial_feedback_file, auth_file, model, reasoning_effort, task_file, instructions_file, memory, cpus, expected_task_sha256, expected_instructions_sha256):
    """Start a NEW run. This command launches the scientist model."""
    if run_dir.exists():
        raise click.ClickException("run directory already exists; use resume")
    if (expected_task_sha256 or expected_instructions_sha256) and not (task_file or instructions_file):
        raise click.ClickException("expected input hashes require custom task/instructions files")
    if agent == "claude" and reasoning_effort not in {"low", "medium", "high", "xhigh", "max"}:
        raise click.ClickException("Claude does not support that reasoning effort")
    try:
        limits = resource_limits(memory, cpus)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise click.ClickException(str(exc)) from exc
    initial_feedback = initial_feedback_file.read_bytes() if initial_feedback_file else None
    if initial_feedback is not None:
        initial_feedback.decode("utf-8")  # Require text, preserve exact bytes below.
    try:
        config = runtime_config(library_root, codex_binary, auth_file, agent, claude_binary)
    except NativeAuthenticationError as exc:
        click.echo(json.dumps({"vcs_client_failure": True, "agent": agent, "failure_kind": "authentication"}))
        raise click.ClickException(str(exc)) from exc
    config.update(model=model or DEFAULT_MODELS[agent], reasoning_effort=reasoning_effort, **limits)
    run = run_dir.resolve()
    (run / "work").mkdir(parents=True)
    (run / "control").mkdir(mode=0o700)
    state = {"version": 1, "status": "new", "cycles": 0, "attempts": [], "responses": [], "runtime": config,
             "protocol_sha256": digest(ROOT / "protocols/v1/instructions.txt"), "task_sha256": digest(ROOT / "protocols/v1/task-balls.txt")}
    # Preserve original scientific input in host custody, outside the agent's workspace.
    if task_file or instructions_file:
        inputs = freeze_protocol(run, task_file or ROOT / "protocols/v1/task-balls.txt",
                                 instructions_file or ROOT / "protocols/v1/instructions.txt",
                                 {"task.txt": expected_task_sha256, "instructions.txt": expected_instructions_sha256})
        config["protocol_snapshot"] = True
        state.update(version=2, protocol_inputs=inputs,
                     protocol_sha256=inputs["instructions.txt"]["sha256"],
                     task_sha256=inputs["task.txt"]["sha256"])
    else:
        shutil.copytree(ROOT / "protocols/v1", run / "control/protocol")
    if initial_feedback is not None:
        atomic_write(run / "control/initial-feedback.txt", initial_feedback)
        state["initial_feedback"] = {"source": "human task clarification supplied via --initial-feedback-file",
                                     "sha256": digest(run / "control/initial-feedback.txt"), "time": stamp(),
                                     "proof_acceptance": False}
    execute_cycle(run, state)


def load_state(run):
    return read_json(run / "control/state.json")


@main.command()
@click.argument("run", type=click.Path(exists=True, path_type=Path))
@click.option("--agent", type=click.Choice(["codex", "claude"]), help="Must match the recorded run client; defaults to that client")
def resume(run, agent):
    """Start a fresh scientist context with preserved attempts and human feedback."""
    run = run.resolve()
    state = load_state(run)
    if agent and agent != client_name(state["runtime"]):
        raise click.ClickException("agent differs from recorded run; start a new run")
    if state["status"] not in {"rejected", "input_received", "check_failed", "scientist_failed", "incomplete"}:
        raise click.ClickException("resume requires rejection, supplied input or a failed/incomplete cycle")
    try:
        protocol_text(run, state)
        validate_runtime(state["runtime"])
        checked_history(run, state)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise click.ClickException(str(exc)) from exc
    execute_cycle(run, state)


@main.command()
@click.argument("run", type=click.Path(exists=True, path_type=Path))
@click.option("--request-id", required=True)
@click.option("--response-file", type=click.Path(exists=True, path_type=Path), required=True)
@click.option("--source", required=True, help="Provenance, method or citation for this external response")
def respond(run, request_id, response_file, source):
    """Supply a real external result or clarification; does not run the model."""
    run = run.resolve()
    state = load_state(run)
    if state["status"] != "awaiting_input" or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{32}", request_id):
        raise click.ClickException("not awaiting that input")
    request = inside(run / "work", "requests/" + request_id + ".json")
    item = {"id": request_id, "request": read_json(request), "response": response_file.read_text(), "source": source, "time": stamp()}
    dest = run / "control" / "responses"
    dest.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    write_json(dest / (request_id + ".json"), item)
    inputs = run / "work/inputs"
    ensure_directory(inputs)
    write_json(inputs / (request_id + ".json"), item)
    state["responses"].append(item)
    request.unlink()
    state["status"] = "awaiting_input" if list(request.parent.glob("*.json")) else "input_received"
    write_json(run / "control/state.json", state)
    click.echo(state["status"])


@main.command()
@click.argument("run", type=click.Path(exists=True, path_type=Path))
@click.option("--decision", type=click.Choice(["accept", "reject"]), required=True)
@click.option("--binding", required=True, help="Exact binding printed by status; human must inspect the packet")
@click.option("--reason", required=True)
def review(run, decision, binding, reason):
    """Human-only decision on an exact immutable review packet; never auto-accepts."""
    run = run.resolve()
    state = load_state(run)
    if state["status"] != "awaiting_review" or not state["attempts"]:
        raise click.ClickException("no verified packet awaiting human review")
    entry = state["attempts"][-1]
    attempt = run / entry["path"]
    actual = content_binding(attempt)
    if binding != entry["binding"] or actual != binding:
        raise click.ClickException("reviewed content changed or binding incorrect; acceptance invalid")
    packet = read_json(attempt / "packet.json")
    if decision == "accept" and not (packet["verification"].get("reviewable") and packet["verification"].get("check_complete")):
        raise click.ClickException("failed checks cannot be accepted")
    if not reason.strip():
        raise click.ClickException("a human review reason is required")
    receipt = {"decision": decision, "binding": binding, "reason": reason, "time": stamp(),
               "verification_verdict": packet["verification"]["verdict"]}
    write_json(attempt / "decision.json", receipt)
    entry["decision_sha256"] = digest(attempt / "decision.json")
    entry["status"] = "accepted" if decision == "accept" else "rejected"
    state["status"] = entry["status"]
    write_json(run / "control/state.json", state)
    write_json(run / "work/human-feedback.json", receipt)
    click.echo(f"{state['status']} ({receipt['verification_verdict']}; human scientific judgment recorded)")


@main.command()
@click.argument("run", type=click.Path(exists=True, path_type=Path))
def status(run):
    """Print human/input checkpoints; invalidate acceptance if artifacts changed."""
    click.echo(json.dumps(status_result(run), indent=2))


def status_result(run):
    state = load_state(run)
    result = {"status": state["status"], "error": state.get("error"), "failure_kind": state.get("failure_kind"),
              "requests": [read_json(inside(run / "work", str(p.relative_to(run / "work"))))
                           for p in (run / "work/requests").glob("*.json")]}
    if state["attempts"]:
        entry = state["attempts"][-1]
        receipt = run / entry["path"] / "decision.json"
        changed = content_binding(run / entry["path"]) != entry["binding"]
        if entry.get("decision_sha256"):
            changed = changed or not receipt.is_file() or digest(receipt) != entry["decision_sha256"]
        if changed:
            state["status"] = "invalidated"
            result["status"] = state["status"]
            write_json(run / "control/state.json", state)
        result.update(attempt=entry, packet=str(run / entry["path"] / "packet.json"))
    return result


@main.group(hidden=True)
def tool():
    """Container-only feedback tools. Host always independently rechecks."""


@tool.command("check")
@click.option("--manifest", type=click.Path(exists=True, path_type=Path), required=True)
@click.option("--out", type=click.Path(path_type=Path))
def tool_check(manifest, out):
    out = out or Path("/work/checks") / uuid.uuid4().hex
    result = verify_candidate(manifest.parent, manifest, out)
    click.echo(json.dumps(result, indent=2))
    if not result["check_complete"]:
        raise click.exceptions.Exit(1)
    if result["verdict"] == "proved_if":
        raise click.exceptions.Exit(3)


@tool.command("request")
@click.option("--kind", type=click.Choice(["literature", "calculation", "experiment", "clarification"]), required=True)
@click.option("--question", required=True)
def tool_request(kind, question):
    folder = Path("/work/requests")
    folder.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    ident = uuid.uuid4().hex
    item = {"id": ident, "kind": kind, "question": question, "time": stamp()}
    write_json(folder / (ident + ".json"), item)
    click.echo(json.dumps(item))
    click.echo("Stop this cycle. A human must supply the actual response before you resume.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

"""Extract statement dependencies and conclusions; independently compile and compare."""
import hashlib
import json

from pathlib import Path
import re

from vcs_lean import LEAN, LEANCHECKER, LISTER, listed  # noqa: E402

# Display text omits implicit arguments, types and sometimes whole subterms.
# Compare the complete kernel expressions instead. Binder names and metadata do
# not affect meaning; remove them so source positions and fresh binder names do
# not make an otherwise identical extraction fail. All constants, universe
# levels, implicit arguments and binder information remain in the identity.
IDENTITY = """
private partial def VAS_identityExpr : Lean.Expr → Lean.Expr
  | .mdata _ value => VAS_identityExpr value
  | .app fn arg => .app (VAS_identityExpr fn) (VAS_identityExpr arg)
  | .lam _ typ body info => .lam .anonymous (VAS_identityExpr typ) (VAS_identityExpr body) info
  | .forallE _ typ body info => .forallE .anonymous (VAS_identityExpr typ) (VAS_identityExpr body) info
  | .letE _ typ value body flag => .letE .anonymous (VAS_identityExpr typ) (VAS_identityExpr value) (VAS_identityExpr body) flag
  | .proj owner idx value => .proj owner idx (VAS_identityExpr value)
  | value => value

private def VAS_declarationIdentity (ci : Lean.ConstantInfo) : String :=
  let kind := match ci with
    | .axiomInfo _ => "axiom"
    | .defnInfo _ => "definition"
    | .thmInfo _ => "theorem"
    | .opaqueInfo _ => "opaque"
    | .quotInfo _ => "quotient"
    | .inductInfo info => s!"inductive {info.numParams} {info.numIndices} {reprStr info.ctors}"
    | .ctorInfo info => s!"constructor {info.induct} {info.cidx} {info.numParams} {info.numFields}"
    | .recInfo info => s!"recursor {info.numParams} {info.numIndices} {info.numMotives} {info.numMinors}"

import Mathlib.Analysis.Calculus.MeanValue
import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.Log.Basic
import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.ExpDeriv
import Mathlib.Tactic.NormNum
import Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith
import Mathlib.Tactic.FieldSimp
import Mathlib.Tactic.Ring
import Mathlib.Tactic.Convert
import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.Trigonometric.Basic

/- Auxiliary explicit curve used AFTER deriving uniqueness from the energy balance.
Conditional lumped heat-balance model. C is total heat capacity (J/K),
G total heat-loss conductance (W/K), t minutes, coffee temperature Celsius.
Ambient 20 C is chosen; initial 90 C is supplied. The 60 converts minutes
into seconds. Constant C and G, uniform temperature and initially hot mug
are modeling assumptions, not observations. -/
noncomputable def coffee (C G t : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  20 + (90 - 20) * Real.exp (-(60 * G / C) * t)

-- For any temperature history obeying the differential energy balance on t>=0,
-- the integrating factor has zero derivative, hence equals its initial value 70.
-- The velocity v is the actual derivative, not a prescribed solution curve.
lemma solution_from_balance : ∀ (T : ℝ → ℝ) (C G : ℝ), 0 < C →
    T 0 = 90 →
    (∀ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t → ∃ v : ℝ, HasDerivAt T v t ∧
      C * v = -60 * G * (T t - 20)) →
    ∀ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t → T t = coffee C G t := by
  intro T C G hC h0 hbal t ht
  let k := 60 * G / C
  let F := fun x : ℝ => (T x - 20) * Real.exp (k * x)
  have hF : ∀ x : ℝ, x ∈ Set.Ici (0 : ℝ) → HasDerivAt F 0 x := by
    intro x hx
    obtain ⟨v, hv, heq⟩ := hbal x hx
    have hvk : v = -k * (T x - 20) := by
      dsimp [k]
      apply (mul_right_cancel₀ (ne_of_gt hC))
      field_simp
      nlinarith [heq]
    have hd := (hv.sub_const 20).mul (((hasDerivAt_id x).const_mul k).exp)
    convert hd using 1
    · rfl
    · dsimp
      rw [hvk]
      ring
  have hn := Convex.norm_image_sub_le_of_norm_hasDerivWithin_le
    (𝕜 := ℝ) (C := 0) (f := F) (f' := fun _ => (0 : ℝ))
    (fun x hx => (hF x hx).hasDerivWithinAt)
    (by intro x hx; simp) (convex_Ici (0 : ℝ))
    (show (0 : ℝ) ∈ Set.Ici (0 : ℝ) by exact le_refl (0 : ℝ)) ht
  have he : F t = F 0 := sub_eq_zero.mp (norm_le_zero_iff.mp (by simpa using hn))
  have hp : (T t - 20) * Real.exp (k * t) = 70 := by
    simpa [F, h0] using he
  have hi : Real.exp (k * t) * Real.exp (-(k * t)) = 1 := by
    rw [← Real.exp_add]
    simp
  have hmul := congrArg (fun z : ℝ => z * Real.exp (-(k * t))) hp
  rw [mul_assoc, hi, mul_one] at hmul
  dsimp [coffee]
  change T t = 20 + (90 - 20) * Real.exp (-k * t)
  rw [neg_mul] at hmul
  linarith

lemma threshold_exponential :
    Real.exp (-Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ)) = (4 / 7 : ℝ) := by
  have h : Real.log (4 / 7 : ℝ) = -Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) := by
    rw [← Real.log_inv]
    norm_num
  rw [← h, Real.exp_log (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 4 / 7)]

-- Solves the threshold equation and proves it is the first downward crossing.
lemma first_crossing : ∀ C G : ℝ, 0 < C → 0 < G →
    (∀ t : ℝ, coffee C G t = 60 ↔
      t = C * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G)) ∧
    (∀ t : ℝ, coffee C G t ≤ 60 ↔
      C * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G) ≤ t) := by
  intro C G hC hG
  have hk : 0 < 60 * G / C := div_pos (by positivity) hC
  have htime : Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G / C) =
      C * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G) := by field_simp
  constructor
  · intro t
    constructor
    · intro h
      have he : Real.exp (-(60 * G / C) * t) = (4 / 7 : ℝ) := by
        dsimp [coffee] at h
        linarith
      have hx := Real.exp_injective (he.trans threshold_exponential.symm)
      rw [← htime]
      apply (eq_div_iff (ne_of_gt hk)).2
      linarith
    · intro h
      rw [← htime] at h
      rw [h]
      have hx : -(60 * G / C) * (Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G / C)) =
          -Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) := by field_simp
      unfold coffee
      rw [hx, threshold_exponential]
      norm_num
  · intro t
    rw [← htime]
  have hC : 0 < C := by dsimp [C]; linarith
  have hG : 0 < G := by linarith
  have hden : 0 < 60 * G := by positivity
  obtain ⟨hLlo, hLhi⟩ := logarithm_bounds
  let tau := C * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G)
  have hlo : 18 < tau := by
    dsimp [tau]
    rw [lt_div_iff₀ hden]
    have hprod := mul_lt_mul_of_pos_left hLlo hC
    change 11382 / 10 ≤ C at hClo
    nlinarith
  have hhi : tau < 59 := by
    dsimp [tau]
    rw [div_lt_iff₀ hden]
    have hprod := mul_lt_mul_of_pos_left hLhi hC
    change C ≤ 12952 / 10 at hChi
    nlinarith
  have hsol := solution_from_balance T C G hC h0 hbal
  obtain ⟨heq, hle⟩ := first_crossing C G hC hG
  refine ⟨tau, hlo, hhi, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro t ht
    rw [hsol t ht]
    exact heq t
  · intro t ht
    rw [hsol t ht]
    exact hle t

/- Nominal masses .25/.20 kg, specific heats 4200/800 J/(kg K),
side/top coefficients 14/35 W/(m^2 K), exact cylindrical areas above.
These are scenario values, not measurements. First crossing: 30..32 minutes. -/
theorem nominal_answer :
    ∀ T : ℝ → ℝ,
    T ((0 : ℕ) : ℝ) = ((90 : ℕ) : ℝ) →
    (∀ t : ℝ, ((0 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ t → ∃ v : ℝ, HasDerivAt T v t ∧
      heatCapacity (((1 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((4 : ℕ) : ℝ)) ((4200 : ℕ) : ℝ)
        (((1 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((5 : ℕ) : ℝ)) ((800 : ℕ) : ℝ) * v =
        -((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) * (((14 : ℕ) : ℝ) * sideArea + ((35 : ℕ) : ℝ) * topArea) *
          (T t - ((20 : ℕ) : ℝ))) →
    ∃ tau : ℝ, ((30 : ℕ) : ℝ) < tau ∧ tau < ((32 : ℕ) : ℝ) ∧
      (∀ t : ℝ, ((0 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ t → T t = ((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) ↔ t = tau) ∧
      (∀ t : ℝ, ((0 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ t → T t ≤ ((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) ↔ tau ≤ t) := by
  intro T h0 hbal
  norm_num only [Nat.cast_ofNat] at h0 hbal ⊢
  obtain ⟨hAslo, hAshi, hAtlo, hAthi⟩ := area_bounds
  let G := 14 * sideArea + 35 * topArea
  have hGlo : (3689 / 10000 : ℝ) ≤ G := by dsimp [G]; linarith
  have hGhi : G ≤ (36967 / 100000 : ℝ) := by dsimp [G]; linarith
  have hG : 0 < G := by linarith
  have hden : 0 < 60 * G := by positivity
  have hcross := first_crossing 1210 G (by norm_num) hG
  obtain ⟨hlo, hhi⟩ := logarithm_bounds
  refine ⟨1210 * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G), ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · rw [lt_div_iff₀ hden]
    nlinarith
  · rw [div_lt_iff₀ hden]
    nlinarith
  · intro t ht
    have hb : ∀ x : ℝ, 0 ≤ x → ∃ v : ℝ, HasDerivAt T v x ∧
        1210 * v = -60 * G * (T x - 20) := by
      simpa [heatCapacity, G] using hbal
    rw [solution_from_balance T 1210 G (by norm_num) h0 hb t ht]
    exact hcross.1 t
  · intro t ht
    have hb : ∀ x : ℝ, 0 ≤ x → ∃ v : ℝ, HasDerivAt T v x ∧
        1210 * v = -60 * G * (T x - 20) := by
      simpa [heatCapacity, G] using hbal
    rw [solution_from_balance T 1210 G (by norm_num) h0 hb t ht]
    exact hcross.2 t

run_cmd do

  let localSet : NameSet := kenv.constants.map₂.toList.foldl (fun s (n, _) => s.insert n) {}

      | .opaqueInfo v => v.value.getUsedConstants
      | .inductInfo v => v.ctors.toArray | .ctorInfo v => #[v.induct] | _ => #[])
    todo := more.toList ++ todo
    -- auxiliary constants (matchers, constructors, projections, equation lemmas) map to the nearest ancestor
    -- whose source range Lean recorded
    let mut m := n
    let mut r ← findDeclarationRanges? m
    while r.isNone && !m.isAnonymous do
      m := m.getPrefix
      r ← findDeclarationRanges? m
    match r with
    | some rg => logInfo m!"VAS_DECL {m} {rg.range.pos.line} {rg.range.endPos.line}"
    | none => logInfo m!"VAS_UNRANGED {n}"
  logInfo "VAS_REACHED"
"""

COMPARE = """
set_option format.width 100000 in
open Lean Elab Command Meta in
run_cmd do
  for n in NAMES do
    let some ci := (← getEnv).find? n | throwError m!"no {n}"
    let some v := ci.value? | throwError m!"no value {n}"
    logInfo m!"VAS_EXTRACTED {n} : {← liftTermElabM <| ppExpr v} VAS_END"
    logInfo m!"VAS_EXTRACTED_IDENTITY {n} : {reprStr (VAS_identityExpr v)} VAS_END"
  for n in DEPENDENCIES do
    let some ci := (← getEnv).find? n | throwError m!"missing extracted dependency {n}"
    logInfo m!"VAS_EXTRACTED_DEPENDENCY_IDENTITY {n} : {VAS_declarationIdentity ci} VAS_END"
"""


def run_lean(path):
        parts.append("\n".join(lines[a - 1:b]) + "\n")
    if current:
        parts.append(f"end {current}\n")
    starts = {}
    for t in theorems:
        m = re.search(r"(?m)^\s*(?:theorem|lemma)\s+" + re.escape(t) + r"\b", src)
        starts[t] = src[:m.start()].count("\n") + 1 if m else None
    stmts = {t: statement_text(lines, starts[t], t) for t in theorems}
    conclusions = "".join(f"def conclusion_{t} : Prop := {stmts[t]}\n" for t in theorems)
    extracted = ("\n".join(imports) + "\n\n" + "\n".join(parts) + "\n\n"
                 "/-! Conclusions (statements of the proved theorems). -/\n" + conclusions)
    out.with_suffix(".lean").write_text(extracted)
    # step 5: the extracted file compiles alone, and its conclusions are the proved statements
    check = out.with_name(out.name + ".check.lean")
    names = "[" + ", ".join(f"``conclusion_{t}" for t in theorems) + "]"
    dependencies = "[" + ", ".join(f"``{name}" for name in dependency_identities) + "]"
    check.write_text(extracted + IDENTITY + COMPARE.replace("NAMES", names).replace("DEPENDENCIES", dependencies))
    status2, text2 = run_lean(check)
    extracted_identities = expression_identities(text2, "VAS_EXTRACTED_IDENTITY")
    same = {t: bool(original_identities.get(t)) and
            original_identities[t] == extracted_identities.get("conclusion_" + t)
            for t in theorems}
    extracted_dependencies = expression_identities(text2, "VAS_EXTRACTED_DEPENDENCY_IDENTITY")
    dependencies_same = {name: value == extracted_dependencies.get(name)
                         for name, value in dependency_identities.items()}
    identity_hashes = {
        t: {"original_sha256": hashlib.sha256(original_identities[t].encode()).hexdigest()
            if t in original_identities else None,
            "extracted_sha256": hashlib.sha256(extracted_identities["conclusion_" + t].encode()).hexdigest()
            if "conclusion_" + t in extracted_identities else None}
        for t in theorems}
    dependency_hashes = {
        name: {"original_sha256": hashlib.sha256(value.encode()).hexdigest(),
               "extracted_sha256": hashlib.sha256(extracted_dependencies[name].encode()).hexdigest()
               if name in extracted_dependencies else None}
        for name, value in dependency_identities.items()}
    report |= {"ok": status2 == 0 and all(same.values()) and all(dependencies_same.values())
               and set(dependency_identities) == set(extracted_dependencies) and not unranged,
               "compiles_alone": status2 == 0,
               "faithful": same, "assumption_declarations": [owner[r] for r in keep],
               "identity_method": "kernel-expression-repr-v1", "conclusion_identities": identity_hashes,
               "dependency_faithful": dependencies_same, "dependency_identities": dependency_hashes,
               "unranged": unranged, "added_assumptions": added,
               "conclusions_as_proved": {t: " ".join(concl[t].split()) for t in theorems},
               "extracted_file": str(out.with_suffix(".lean")),
               "errors": [ln for ln in text2.splitlines() if ": error" in ln][:5]}
    out.with_suffix(".json").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=1))
    probe.unlink()
    check.unlink()
    print(json.dumps(report, indent=1))
    sys.exit(0 if report["ok"] else 1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

attempt-004/Model.lean
import Mathlib.Analysis.Calculus.MeanValue
import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.Log.Basic
import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.ExpDeriv
import Mathlib.Tactic.NormNum
import Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith
import Mathlib.Tactic.FieldSimp
import Mathlib.Tactic.Ring
import Mathlib.Tactic.Convert
import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.Trigonometric.Basic

-- Library-derived regularity fact, named only to keep the derivative statements
-- identical under the harness's independent re-elaboration; no physical premise.
lemma real_scalar_multiplication_continuous : ContinuousSMul ℝ ℝ := by
  infer_instance

/- Auxiliary explicit curve used AFTER deriving uniqueness from the energy balance.
Conditional lumped heat-balance model. C is total heat capacity (J/K),
G total heat-loss conductance (W/K), t minutes, coffee temperature Celsius.
Ambient 20 C is chosen; initial 90 C is supplied. The 60 converts minutes
into seconds. Constant C and G, uniform temperature and initially hot mug
are modeling assumptions, not observations. -/
noncomputable def coffee (C G t : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  20 + (90 - 20) * Real.exp (-(60 * G / C) * t)

-- For any temperature history obeying the differential energy balance on t>=0,
-- the integrating factor has zero derivative, hence equals its initial value 70.
-- The velocity v is the actual derivative, not a prescribed solution curve.
lemma solution_from_balance : ∀ (T : ℝ → ℝ) (C G : ℝ), 0 < C →
    T 0 = 90 →
    (∀ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t → ∃ v : ℝ, @HasDerivAt ℝ _ ℝ _ _ _ real_scalar_multiplication_continuous T v t ∧
      C * v = -60 * G * (T t - 20)) →
    ∀ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t → T t = coffee C G t := by
  intro T C G hC h0 hbal t ht
  let k := 60 * G / C
  let F := fun x : ℝ => (T x - 20) * Real.exp (k * x)
  have hF : ∀ x : ℝ, x ∈ Set.Ici (0 : ℝ) → HasDerivAt F 0 x := by
    intro x hx
    obtain ⟨v, hv, heq⟩ := hbal x hx
    have hvk : v = -k * (T x - 20) := by
      dsimp [k]
      apply (mul_right_cancel₀ (ne_of_gt hC))
      field_simp
      nlinarith [heq]
    have hd := (hv.sub_const 20).mul (((hasDerivAt_id x).const_mul k).exp)
    convert hd using 1
    · rfl
    · dsimp
      rw [hvk]
      ring
  have hn := Convex.norm_image_sub_le_of_norm_hasDerivWithin_le
    (𝕜 := ℝ) (C := 0) (f := F) (f' := fun _ => (0 : ℝ))
    (fun x hx => (hF x hx).hasDerivWithinAt)
    (by intro x hx; simp) (convex_Ici (0 : ℝ))
    (show (0 : ℝ) ∈ Set.Ici (0 : ℝ) by exact le_refl (0 : ℝ)) ht
  have he : F t = F 0 := sub_eq_zero.mp (norm_le_zero_iff.mp (by simpa using hn))
  have hp : (T t - 20) * Real.exp (k * t) = 70 := by
    have he' : (T t - 20) * Real.exp (k * t) = 90 - 20 := by
      simpa [F, h0] using he
    linarith
  have hi : Real.exp (k * t) * Real.exp (-(k * t)) = 1 := by
    rw [← Real.exp_add]
    simp
  have hmul := congrArg (fun z : ℝ => z * Real.exp (-(k * t))) hp
  rw [mul_assoc, hi, mul_one] at hmul
  dsimp [coffee]
  change T t = 20 + (90 - 20) * Real.exp (-k * t)
  rw [← neg_mul] at hmul
  linarith

lemma threshold_exponential :
    Real.exp (-Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ)) = (4 / 7 : ℝ) := by
  have h : Real.log (4 / 7 : ℝ) = -Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) := by
    rw [← Real.log_inv]
    norm_num
  rw [← h, Real.exp_log (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 4 / 7)]

-- Solves the threshold equation and proves it is the first downward crossing.
lemma first_crossing : ∀ C G : ℝ, 0 < C → 0 < G →
    (∀ t : ℝ, coffee C G t = 60 ↔
      t = C * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G)) ∧
    (∀ t : ℝ, coffee C G t ≤ 60 ↔
      C * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G) ≤ t) := by
  intro C G hC hG
  have hk : 0 < 60 * G / C := div_pos (by positivity) hC
  have htime : Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G / C) =
      C * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G) := by field_simp
  constructor
  · intro t
    constructor
    · intro h
      have he : Real.exp (-(60 * G / C) * t) = (4 / 7 : ℝ) := by
        dsimp [coffee] at h
        linarith
      have hx := Real.exp_injective (he.trans threshold_exponential.symm)
      rw [← htime]
      apply (eq_div_iff (ne_of_gt hk)).2
      linarith
    · intro h
      rw [← htime] at h
      rw [h]
      have hx : -(60 * G / C) * (Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G / C)) =
          -Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) := by field_simp
      unfold coffee
      rw [hx, threshold_exponential]
      norm_num
  · intro t
    rw [← htime]
    calc
      coffee C G t ≤ 60 ↔
        Real.exp (-(60 * G / C) * t) ≤ Real.exp (-Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ)) := by
          rw [threshold_exponential]
          unfold coffee
          constructor <;> intro h <;> linarith
      _ ↔ -(60 * G / C) * t ≤ -Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) := Real.exp_le_exp
      _ ↔ Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G / C) ≤ t := by
        rw [div_le_iff₀ hk]
        constructor <;> intro h <;> linarith

-- Certified logarithm bounds, derived from log x <= x-1 and monotonicity.
-- No decimal logarithm or numerical cooling time is assumed.
lemma logarithm_bounds : (55 / 100 : ℝ) < Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) ∧
    Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) < (57 / 100 : ℝ) := by
  have hu := Real.log_lt_log (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 7 / 4)
    (by norm_num : (7 / 4 : ℝ) < (1019 / 1000 : ℝ) ^ 30)
  have hu' := Real.log_le_sub_one_of_pos (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 1019 / 1000)
  rw [Real.log_pow] at hu
  have hl := Real.log_lt_log (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 4 / 7)
    (by norm_num : (4 / 7 : ℝ) < (589 / 600 : ℝ) ^ 30)
  have hl' := Real.log_le_sub_one_of_pos (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 589 / 600)
  rw [Real.log_pow] at hl
  have hi : Real.log (4 / 7 : ℝ) = -Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) := by
    rw [← Real.log_inv]
    norm_num
  rw [hi] at hl
  constructor <;> linarith

/- Chosen dimensions in metres: internal radius .04, wall .004,
liquid and exposed side height .05. Only the side adjacent to liquid loses
heat in this approximation; rim, handle, base and wall above liquid are neglected.
Areas are exact cylinder formulae, not independently assigned rounded values. -/
noncomputable def sideArea : ℝ := 2 * Real.pi * (4 / 100 + 4 / 1000) * (5 / 100)
noncomputable def topArea : ℝ := Real.pi * (4 / 100) ^ 2

-- Combined thermal storage: masses kg, specific heats J/(kg K), result J/K.
def heatCapacity (mc cc mm cm : ℝ) : ℝ := mc * cc + mm * cm

-- Derived capacity bounds, not additional assumptions about total C.
lemma capacity_bounds : ∀ mc cc mm cm : ℝ,
    24 / 100 ≤ mc → mc ≤ 26 / 100 →
    4180 ≤ cc → cc ≤ 4220 →
    18 / 100 ≤ mm → mm ≤ 22 / 100 →
    750 ≤ cm → cm ≤ 900 →
    11382 / 10 ≤ heatCapacity mc cc mm cm ∧
    heatCapacity mc cc mm cm ≤ 12952 / 10 := by
  intro mc cc mm cm hmcl hmcu hccl hccu hmml hmmu hcml hcmu
  have hmc : 0 ≤ mc := by linarith
  have hmm : 0 ≤ mm := by linarith
  have hlc := mul_le_mul hmcl hccl (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 4180) hmc
  have hlm := mul_le_mul hmml hcml (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 750) hmm
  have huc := mul_le_mul hmcu hccu (by linarith : 0 ≤ cc)
    (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 26 / 100)
  have hum := mul_le_mul hmmu hcmu (by linarith : 0 ≤ cm)
    (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 22 / 100)
  dsimp [heatCapacity]
  constructor <;> nlinarith

-- Technical square-root enclosures used only to certify geometric arithmetic.
lemma sqrt_enclosure : ∀ x a b : ℝ, 0 ≤ a → 0 ≤ b → a^2 ≤ x → x ≤ b^2 →
    a ≤ Real.sqrt x ∧ Real.sqrt x ≤ b := by
  intro x a b ha hb hl hu
  have hx : 0 ≤ x := by nlinarith [sq_nonneg a]
  have hs := Real.sq_sqrt hx
  have hn := Real.sqrt_nonneg x
  constructor <;> nlinarith

-- Pi bounds are proved from the installed trigonometric Taylor remainder,
-- not supplied as numerical assumptions.
lemma pi_bounds : (314 / 100 : ℝ) < Real.pi ∧ Real.pi < (3142 / 1000 : ℝ) := by
  obtain ⟨hl0, hu0⟩ := sqrt_enclosure (2 : ℝ) (14142135623 / 10000000000) (14142135624 / 10000000000) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num)
  obtain ⟨hl1, hu1⟩ := sqrt_enclosure (2 + Real.sqrt 2) (18477590650 / 10000000000) (18477590651 / 10000000000) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num; linarith [hl0, hu0]) (by norm_num; linarith [hl0, hu0])
  obtain ⟨hl2, hu2⟩ := sqrt_enclosure (2 + Real.sqrt (2 + Real.sqrt 2)) (19615705608 / 10000000000) (19615705609 / 10000000000) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num; linarith [hl1, hu1]) (by norm_num; linarith [hl1, hu1])
  obtain ⟨hl3, hu3⟩ := sqrt_enclosure (2 - Real.sqrt (2 + Real.sqrt (2 + Real.sqrt 2))) (1960342700 / 10000000000) (1960343000 / 10000000000) (by norm_num) (by norm_num) (by norm_num; linarith [hl2, hu2]) (by norm_num; linarith [hl2, hu2])
  have hs : (9801713 / 100000000 : ℝ) ≤ Real.sin (Real.pi / 32) ∧
      Real.sin (Real.pi / 32) ≤ (9801715 / 100000000 : ℝ) := by
    rw [Real.sin_pi_div_thirty_two]
    constructor <;> linarith
  have hp := Real.pi_pos
  have hp4 := Real.pi_le_four
  have hx : |Real.pi / 32| ≤ 1 := by rw [abs_of_pos (by positivity)]; linarith
  have he := Real.sin_bound hx
  rw [abs_of_pos (by positivity : 0 < Real.pi / 32)] at he
  obtain ⟨hel, heu⟩ := abs_le.mp he
  have hsmall : 0 ≤ Real.pi / 32 ∧ Real.pi / 32 ≤ 1 / 8 := by constructor <;> linarith
  have hcubic : (Real.pi / 32)^3 ≤ (1 / 8 : ℝ)^3 := pow_le_pow_left₀ hsmall.1 hsmall.2 3
  have hfifth : (Real.pi / 32)^5 ≤ (1 / 8 : ℝ)^5 := pow_le_pow_left₀ hsmall.1 hsmall.2 5
  have hp315 : Real.pi < 315 / 100 := by nlinarith [hs.2]
  have hxhi : Real.pi / 32 ≤ (1 / 10 : ℝ) := by linarith
  have hcubic2 : (Real.pi / 32)^3 ≤ (1 / 10 : ℝ)^3 := pow_le_pow_left₀ hsmall.1 hxhi 3
  have hfifth2 : (Real.pi / 32)^5 ≤ (1 / 10 : ℝ)^5 := pow_le_pow_left₀ hsmall.1 hxhi 5
  have hxlo : (98 / 1000 : ℝ) ≤ Real.pi / 32 := by nlinarith [hs.1, pow_nonneg hsmall.1 3]
  have hcubiclo : (98 / 1000 : ℝ)^3 ≤ (Real.pi / 32)^3 :=
    pow_le_pow_left₀ (by norm_num) hxlo 3
  constructor <;> nlinarith [hs.1, hs.2]

-- Rounded enclosures are proved to contain the exact cylindrical areas.
lemma area_bounds :
    (138 / 10000 : ℝ) ≤ sideArea ∧ sideArea ≤ (1383 / 100000 : ℝ) ∧
    (502 / 100000 : ℝ) ≤ topArea ∧ topArea ≤ (503 / 100000 : ℝ) := by
  obtain ⟨hlo, hhi⟩ := pi_bounds
  dsimp [sideArea, topArea]
  norm_num at hlo hhi ⊢
  constructor
  · linarith
  constructor
  · linarith
  constructor <;> linarith

/- Conditional uncertainty result: all masses, specific heats and coefficients
below are explicit scenario assumptions. Combined C and G bounds are derived.
Uniform preheated coffee+mug, ambient 20 C, constant effective coefficients.
The unique first 60 C crossing is between 18 and 59 minutes. -/
theorem scenario_answer :
    ∀ (T : ℝ → ℝ) (mc cc mm cm hs ht : ℝ),
    ((24 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((100 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ mc → mc ≤ ((26 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((100 : ℕ) : ℝ) →
    ((4180 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ cc → cc ≤ ((4220 : ℕ) : ℝ) →
    ((18 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((100 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ mm → mm ≤ ((22 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((100 : ℕ) : ℝ) →
    ((750 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ cm → cm ≤ ((900 : ℕ) : ℝ) →
    ((8 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ hs → hs ≤ ((16 : ℕ) : ℝ) → ((20 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ ht → ht ≤ ((70 : ℕ) : ℝ) →
    T ((0 : ℕ) : ℝ) = ((90 : ℕ) : ℝ) →
    (∀ t : ℝ, ((0 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ t → ∃ v : ℝ, @HasDerivAt ℝ _ ℝ _ _ _ real_scalar_multiplication_continuous T v t ∧
      heatCapacity mc cc mm cm * v =
        -((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) * (hs * sideArea + ht * topArea) * (T t - ((20 : ℕ) : ℝ))) →
    ∃ tau : ℝ, ((18 : ℕ) : ℝ) < tau ∧ tau < ((59 : ℕ) : ℝ) ∧
      (∀ t : ℝ, ((0 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ t → (T t = ((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) ↔ t = tau)) ∧
      (∀ t : ℝ, ((0 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ t → (T t ≤ ((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) ↔ tau ≤ t)) := by
  intro T mc cc mm cm hs ht hmcl hmcu hccl hccu hmml hmmu hcml hcmu hslo hshi htlo hthi h0 hbal
  norm_num only [Nat.cast_ofNat] at hmcl hmcu hccl hccu hmml hmmu hcml hcmu hslo hshi htlo hthi h0 hbal ⊢
  obtain ⟨hClo, hChi⟩ := capacity_bounds mc cc mm cm (by linarith) (by linarith) hccl hccu (by linarith) (by linarith) hcml hcmu
  obtain ⟨hAslo, hAshi, hAtlo, hAthi⟩ := area_bounds
  let C := heatCapacity mc cc mm cm
  let G := hs * sideArea + ht * topArea
  have hAs : 0 ≤ sideArea := by linarith
  have hAt : 0 ≤ topArea := by linarith
  have hsl := mul_le_mul hslo hAslo (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 138 / 10000)
    (by linarith : 0 ≤ hs)
  have htl := mul_le_mul htlo hAtlo (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 502 / 100000)
    (by linarith : 0 ≤ ht)
  have hsu := mul_le_mul hshi hAshi hAs (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 16)
  have htu := mul_le_mul hthi hAthi hAt (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 70)
  have hGlo : (2108 / 10000 : ℝ) ≤ G := by dsimp [G]; linarith
  have hGhi : G ≤ (57338 / 100000 : ℝ) := by dsimp [G]; linarith
  have hC : 0 < C := by dsimp [C]; linarith
  have hG : 0 < G := by linarith
  have hden : 0 < 60 * G := by positivity
  obtain ⟨hLlo, hLhi⟩ := logarithm_bounds
  let tau := C * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G)
  have hlo : 18 < tau := by
    dsimp [tau]
    rw [lt_div_iff₀ hden]
    have hprod := mul_lt_mul_of_pos_left hLlo hC
    change 11382 / 10 ≤ C at hClo
    nlinarith
  have hhi : tau < 59 := by
    dsimp [tau]
    rw [div_lt_iff₀ hden]
    have hprod := mul_lt_mul_of_pos_left hLhi hC
    change C ≤ 12952 / 10 at hChi
    nlinarith
  have hsol := solution_from_balance T C G hC h0 hbal
  obtain ⟨heq, hle⟩ := first_crossing C G hC hG
  refine ⟨tau, hlo, hhi, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro t ht
    rw [hsol t ht]
    exact heq t
  · intro t ht
    rw [hsol t ht]
    exact hle t

/- Nominal masses .25/.20 kg, specific heats 4200/800 J/(kg K),
side/top coefficients 14/35 W/(m^2 K), exact cylindrical areas above.
These are scenario values, not measurements. First crossing: 30..32 minutes. -/
theorem nominal_answer :
    ∀ T : ℝ → ℝ,
    T ((0 : ℕ) : ℝ) = ((90 : ℕ) : ℝ) →
    (∀ t : ℝ, ((0 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ t → ∃ v : ℝ, @HasDerivAt ℝ _ ℝ _ _ _ real_scalar_multiplication_continuous T v t ∧
      heatCapacity (((1 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((4 : ℕ) : ℝ)) ((4200 : ℕ) : ℝ)
        (((1 : ℕ) : ℝ) / ((5 : ℕ) : ℝ)) ((800 : ℕ) : ℝ) * v =
        -((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) * (((14 : ℕ) : ℝ) * sideArea + ((35 : ℕ) : ℝ) * topArea) *
          (T t - ((20 : ℕ) : ℝ))) →
    ∃ tau : ℝ, ((30 : ℕ) : ℝ) < tau ∧ tau < ((32 : ℕ) : ℝ) ∧
      (∀ t : ℝ, ((0 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ t → (T t = ((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) ↔ t = tau)) ∧
      (∀ t : ℝ, ((0 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ t → (T t ≤ ((60 : ℕ) : ℝ) ↔ tau ≤ t)) := by
  intro T h0 hbal
  norm_num only [Nat.cast_ofNat] at h0 hbal ⊢
  obtain ⟨hAslo, hAshi, hAtlo, hAthi⟩ := area_bounds
  let G := 14 * sideArea + 35 * topArea
  have hGlo : (3689 / 10000 : ℝ) ≤ G := by dsimp [G]; linarith
  have hGhi : G ≤ (36967 / 100000 : ℝ) := by dsimp [G]; linarith
  have hG : 0 < G := by linarith
  have hden : 0 < 60 * G := by positivity
  have hb : ∀ x : ℝ, 0 ≤ x → ∃ v : ℝ, HasDerivAt T v x ∧
      1210 * v = -60 * G * (T x - 20) := by
    norm_num [heatCapacity] at hbal
    simpa only [G, neg_mul] using hbal
  have hsol := solution_from_balance T 1210 G (by norm_num) h0 hb
  have hcross := first_crossing 1210 G (by norm_num) hG
  obtain ⟨hlo, hhi⟩ := logarithm_bounds
  refine ⟨1210 * Real.log (7 / 4 : ℝ) / (60 * G), ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · rw [lt_div_iff₀ hden]
    nlinarith
  · rw [div_lt_iff₀ hden]
    nlinarith
  · intro t ht
    rw [hsol t ht]
    exact hcross.1 t
  · intro t ht
    rw [hsol t ht]
    exact hcross.2 t

import Mathlib.Analysis.Calculus.MeanValue

import Mathlib.Tactic.FieldSimp
